package com.replayer.data

import android.content.Context
import android.content.SharedPreferences
import android.graphics.BitmapFactory
import com.replayer.model.MediaItem
import com.replayer.model.MediaSourceType
import com.replayer.model.MediaType
import com.replayer.model.RecentMedia
import com.replayer.network.upnp.ContentDirectoryService
import kotlinx.coroutines.CoroutineScope
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.asStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext
import kotlinx.serialization.Serializable
import kotlinx.serialization.encodeToString
import kotlinx.serialization.json.Json
import okhttp3.OkHttpClient
import okhttp3.Request
import org.json.JSONObject
import java.net.URLEncoder
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap
import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit

@Serializable
data class MediaWatchProgress(
    val positionMs: Long = 0L,
    val durationMs: Long = 0L,
    val updatedAt: Long = System.currentTimeMillis()
) {
    fun progress(): Float {
        if (durationMs <= 0L || positionMs <= 0L) return 0f
        return (positionMs.toFloat() / durationMs.toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
    }
}

class RecentMediaManager private constructor(context: Context) {

    companion object {
        @Volatile
        private var INSTANCE: RecentMediaManager? = null

        fun getInstance(context: Context): RecentMediaManager {
            return INSTANCE ?: synchronized(this) {
                INSTANCE ?: RecentMediaManager(context.applicationContext).also { INSTANCE = it }
            }
        }
    }

    private val prefs: SharedPreferences =
        context.getSharedPreferences("replayer_recent_media", Context.MODE_PRIVATE)

    private val json = Json { ignoreUnknownKeys = true }
    private val scope = CoroutineScope(Dispatchers.IO)
    private val httpClient = OkHttpClient.Builder()
        .connectTimeout(5, TimeUnit.SECONDS)
        .readTimeout(5, TimeUnit.SECONDS)
        .followRedirects(true)
        .build()

    private val _recentList = MutableStateFlow<List<RecentMedia>>(loadRecent())
    val recentList: StateFlow<List<RecentMedia>> = _recentList.asStateFlow()

    private val _progressMap = MutableStateFlow<Map<String, MediaWatchProgress>>(loadProgressMap())
    val progressMap: StateFlow<Map<String, MediaWatchProgress>> = _progressMap.asStateFlow()

    private val listLock = Any()
    private val attemptedPosterKeys = HashSet<String>()
    private val scanningKeys = HashSet<String>()
    private val previewPosterCache = ConcurrentHashMap<String, String>()
    private val folderLastOpened: MutableMap<String, String> = loadFolderLastOpened()

    private fun loadFolderLastOpened(): MutableMap<String, String> {
        val raw = prefs.getString("folder_last_opened_json", null) ?: return mutableMapOf()
        return try {
            json.decodeFromString<Map<String, String>>(raw).toMutableMap()
        } catch (_: Exception) {
            mutableMapOf()
        }
    }

    private fun saveFolderLastOpenedLocked() {
        try {
            if (folderLastOpened.size > 400) {
                val keysToDrop = folderLastOpened.keys.take(folderLastOpened.size - 400)
                keysToDrop.forEach { folderLastOpened.remove(it) }
            }
            prefs.edit().putString("folder_last_opened_json", json.encodeToString(folderLastOpened.toMap())).apply()
        } catch (_: Exception) {}
    }

    private fun recordFolderSelectionLocked(folderId: String?, itemId: String, itemUri: String) {
        val cleanFolder = folderId?.ifBlank { null } ?: return
        if (itemId.isBlank()) return
        // Re-insert so LinkedHashMap preserves most-recently-used ordering at the tail
        folderLastOpened.remove(cleanFolder)
        folderLastOpened[cleanFolder] = "$itemId\n$itemUri"
        saveFolderLastOpenedLocked()
    }

    fun recordFolderSelection(folderId: String?, itemId: String, itemUri: String) {
        synchronized(listLock) {
            recordFolderSelectionLocked(folderId, itemId, itemUri)
        }
    }

    fun getLastOpenedKeyInFolder(folderId: String?): Pair<String, String>? {
        val cleanFolder = folderId?.ifBlank { null } ?: return null
        val stored = synchronized(listLock) { folderLastOpened[cleanFolder] } ?: return null
        val parts = stored.split('\n', limit = 2)
        val id = parts.getOrNull(0)?.ifBlank { null } ?: return null
        val uri = parts.getOrNull(1) ?: ""
        return Pair(id, uri)
    }

    private fun loadRecent(): List<RecentMedia> {
        val raw = prefs.getString("recent_media_json", null) ?: return emptyList()
        return try {
            val loaded = json.decodeFromString<List<RecentMedia>>(raw)
            // Enforce one item per folder & max 2 items
            val seenFolders = mutableSetOf<String>()
            loaded.filter { item ->
                val folderKey = item.parentId?.ifBlank { null } ?: item.id
                seenFolders.add(folderKey)
            }.take(2)
        } catch (_: Exception) {
            emptyList()
        }
    }

    private fun saveRecent(list: List<RecentMedia>) {
        val trimmed = list.take(2)
        _recentList.value = trimmed
        try {
            val encoded = json.encodeToString(trimmed)
            prefs.edit().putString("recent_media_json", encoded).apply()
        } catch (_: Exception) {}
    }

    private fun loadProgressMap(): Map<String, MediaWatchProgress> {
        val raw = prefs.getString("watch_progress_json", null) ?: return emptyMap()
        return try {
            json.decodeFromString<Map<String, MediaWatchProgress>>(raw)
        } catch (_: Exception) {
            emptyMap()
        }
    }

    private fun saveProgressMap(map: Map<String, MediaWatchProgress>) {
        val trimmed = if (map.size > 300) {
            map.entries
                .sortedByDescending { it.value.updatedAt }
                .take(300)
                .associate { it.key to it.value }
        } else map

        _progressMap.value = trimmed
        try {
            prefs.edit().putString("watch_progress_json", json.encodeToString(trimmed)).apply()
        } catch (_: Exception) {}
    }

    fun addRecent(
        id: String,
        title: String,
        uri: String,
        parentId: String? = null,
        folderTitle: String? = null,
        thumbnailUrl: String? = null
    ) {
        synchronized(listLock) {
            val current = _recentList.value.toMutableList()
            val cleanParent = parentId?.ifBlank { null }
            val sameExact = current.find { it.id == id || it.uri == uri }
            val sameFolder = if (cleanParent != null) current.find { it.parentId == cleanParent } else null

            val savedProg = _progressMap.value[id] ?: _progressMap.value[uri]
            val pos = sameExact?.positionMs?.takeIf { it > 0L } ?: savedProg?.positionMs ?: 0L
            val dur = sameExact?.durationMs?.takeIf { it > 0L } ?: savedProg?.durationMs ?: 0L

            val resolvedThumb = thumbnailUrl?.ifBlank { null }
                ?: sameExact?.thumbnailUrl?.ifBlank { null }
                ?: sameFolder?.thumbnailUrl?.ifBlank { null }

            // Deduplicate: keep only ONE entry per folder (or per item if parentId unknown)
            current.removeAll { item ->
                item.id == id || item.uri == uri || (cleanParent != null && item.parentId == cleanParent)
            }
            current.add(
                0,
                RecentMedia(
                    id = id,
                    title = title,
                    uri = uri,
                    timestamp = System.currentTimeMillis(),
                    positionMs = pos,
                    durationMs = dur,
                    thumbnailUrl = resolvedThumb,
                    parentId = cleanParent,
                    folderTitle = folderTitle ?: sameFolder?.folderTitle
                )
            )
            // Remember last played file inside its DLNA folder for smart cursor restoration
            if (cleanParent != null) {
                recordFolderSelectionLocked(cleanParent, id, uri)
            }
            // Limit section strictly to 2 folders
            saveRecent(current.take(2))
        }
    }

    fun updateThumbnail(id: String, uri: String, thumbnailUrl: String, parentId: String? = null) {
        synchronized(listLock) {
            val current = _recentList.value.toMutableList()
            var changed = false
            val cleanParent = parentId?.ifBlank { null }
            for (i in current.indices) {
                val item = current[i]
                if (item.id == id || item.uri == uri || (cleanParent != null && item.parentId == cleanParent)) {
                    if (item.thumbnailUrl != thumbnailUrl) {
                        current[i] = item.copy(thumbnailUrl = thumbnailUrl)
                        changed = true
                    }
                }
            }
            if (changed) {
                saveRecent(current)
            }
        }
    }

    fun getCachedPreviewPoster(itemId: String): String? =
        previewPosterCache[itemId]?.ifBlank { null }

    fun hasCachedPreviewPoster(itemId: String): Boolean =
        previewPosterCache.containsKey(itemId)

    /**
     * Resolves a vertical poster preview for DLNA list/folder inspection with memory caching.
     * Generic top-level category folders (e.g. "Видео", "Фильмы") skip online lookups to avoid false hits.
     */
    suspend fun resolvePreviewPosterForItem(
        item: MediaItem,
        folderItems: List<MediaItem>,
        controlUrl: String? = null,
        folderTitle: String? = null
    ): String? {
        previewPosterCache[item.id]?.let { return it.ifBlank { null } }

        return withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
            previewPosterCache[item.id]?.let { return@withContext it.ifBlank { null } }

            val resolved: String? = when (item.type) {
                MediaType.IMAGE -> item.uri.ifBlank { item.thumbnailUrl?.ifBlank { null } }
                MediaType.AUDIO -> item.thumbnailUrl?.ifBlank { null }
                MediaType.DIRECTORY -> {
                    val lower = item.title.trim().lowercase()
                    if (isGenericCategoryFolder(lower)) {
                        item.thumbnailUrl?.ifBlank { null }
                    } else {
                        val isSeasonSub = Regex("^(season|sezon|сезон|s\\d+|part|часть)\\s*\\d*$", RegexOption.IGNORE_CASE)
                            .matches(item.title.trim())
                        val effectiveTitle = if (isSeasonSub && !folderTitle.isNullOrBlank() && !isGenericCategoryFolder(folderTitle.trim().lowercase())) {
                            folderTitle
                        } else {
                            item.title
                        }
                        val subItems = if (!controlUrl.isNullOrBlank()) {
                            try {
                                ContentDirectoryService().browse(controlUrl, item.id)
                            } catch (_: Throwable) {
                                emptyList()
                            }
                        } else emptyList()

                        findVerticalPosterCascade(
                            targetItem = item,
                            folderItems = subItems,
                            controlUrl = controlUrl,
                            folderTitle = effectiveTitle
                        )
                    }
                }
                MediaType.VIDEO, MediaType.UNKNOWN -> {
                    // Instant reuse if already resolved for Continue Watching
                    val fromRecent = _recentList.value.firstOrNull {
                        (it.id == item.id || it.uri == item.uri || (!item.parentId.isNullOrBlank() && it.parentId == item.parentId)) &&
                            !it.thumbnailUrl.isNullOrBlank()
                    }?.thumbnailUrl
                    if (!fromRecent.isNullOrBlank()) {
                        fromRecent
                    } else {
                        val effectiveFolder = folderTitle?.takeIf { !isGenericCategoryFolder(it.trim().lowercase()) }
                        findVerticalPosterCascade(
                            targetItem = item,
                            folderItems = folderItems,
                            controlUrl = controlUrl,
                            folderTitle = effectiveFolder
                        )
                    }
                }
            }

            previewPosterCache[item.id] = resolved ?: ""
            resolved
        }
    }

    private fun isGenericCategoryFolder(lowerTitle: String): Boolean {
        if (lowerTitle.length <= 2) return true
        val generic = setOf(
            "видео", "фильмы", "сериалы", "папки", "фото", "изображения", "картинки",
            "музыка", "аудио", "все видео", "все файлы", "все фото", "недавнее", "недавние",
            "новые", "добавленные", "избранное", "загрузки", "торренты", "downloads",
            "video", "videos", "movies", "films", "tv", "series", "folders", "by folder",
            "all video", "all files", "all", "photos", "pictures", "images", "music", "audio",
            "recent", "recently added", "favorites"
        )
        return generic.contains(lowerTitle)
    }

    /**
     * Searches files in the same DLNA folder (and across image branches of the DLNA server,
     * with online artwork fallback) for a vertical (portrait, height > width) poster.
     */
    fun resolveVerticalPoster(
        targetItem: MediaItem,
        folderItems: List<MediaItem>,
        controlUrl: String? = null,
        folderTitle: String? = null
    ) {
        scope.launch {
            val posterUrl = findVerticalPosterCascade(
                targetItem = targetItem,
                folderItems = folderItems,
                controlUrl = controlUrl,
                folderTitle = folderTitle
            )
            if (posterUrl != null) {
                updateThumbnail(targetItem.id, targetItem.uri, posterUrl, targetItem.parentId)
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * Scans recent items missing a thumbnail (on HomeScreen start or when UPnP device connects)
     * to resolve vertical posters via DLNA folder, DLNA image/search branches, or online poster fallback.
     */
    fun resolveMissingPosters(controlUrl: String?) {
        val targets = _recentList.value.filter { recent ->
            recent.thumbnailUrl.isNullOrBlank()
        }
        if (targets.isEmpty()) return

        val cds = ContentDirectoryService()
        for (recent in targets) {
            val pId = recent.parentId?.ifBlank { null }
            val cacheKey = "${recent.id}|${pId.orEmpty()}|${controlUrl.orEmpty()}"
            val shouldScan = synchronized(listLock) {
                if (attemptedPosterKeys.contains(cacheKey) || scanningKeys.contains(cacheKey)) {
                    false
                } else {
                    scanningKeys.add(cacheKey)
                    true
                }
            }
            if (!shouldScan) continue

            scope.launch {
                try {
                    val folderItems = if (!controlUrl.isNullOrBlank() && pId != null) {
                        cds.browse(controlUrl, pId)
                    } else {
                        emptyList()
                    }
                    val targetAsMedia = MediaItem(
                        id = recent.id,
                        title = recent.title,
                        uri = recent.uri,
                        type = MediaType.VIDEO,
                        sourceType = MediaSourceType.UPNP_DLNA,
                        parentId = pId
                    )
                    val posterUrl = findVerticalPosterCascade(
                        targetItem = targetAsMedia,
                        folderItems = folderItems,
                        controlUrl = controlUrl,
                        folderTitle = recent.folderTitle
                    )
                    if (posterUrl != null) {
                        updateThumbnail(recent.id, recent.uri, posterUrl, pId)
                    }
                    synchronized(listLock) {
                        attemptedPosterKeys.add(cacheKey)
                    }
                } catch (_: Throwable) {
                } finally {
                    synchronized(listLock) {
                        scanningKeys.remove(cacheKey)
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    private suspend fun findVerticalPosterCascade(
        targetItem: MediaItem,
        folderItems: List<MediaItem>,
        controlUrl: String?,
        folderTitle: String?
    ): String? {
        // 1. Local image inside the current folder
        findVerticalPosterInList(targetItem, folderItems)?.let { return it }

        // 2. Fallback: server-provided albumArtURI on the video item itself
        val art = targetItem.thumbnailUrl
        if (!art.isNullOrBlank() && isVerticalImage(art, null)) {
            return art
        }

        // 3. DLNA servers like MiniDLNA/Keenetic hide images inside "Video" folders, placing the same
        // folder under "Pictures/Изображения" instead. Search the DLNA server across image branches!
        if (!controlUrl.isNullOrBlank()) {
            findPosterAcrossUpnpServer(
                controlUrl = controlUrl,
                targetItem = targetItem,
                folderTitle = folderTitle
            )?.let { return it }
        }

        // 4. Last-resort fallback: vertical poster via TVMaze / iTunes by clean title
        return findOnlineVerticalPoster(folderTitle, targetItem.title)
    }

    private fun findVerticalPosterInList(targetItem: MediaItem, folderItems: List<MediaItem>): String? {
        if (folderItems.isEmpty()) return null
        val videoStem = targetItem.title.substringBeforeLast('.').lowercase().trim()
        val images = folderItems
            .filter { item ->
                item.uri.isNotBlank() && item.type != MediaType.DIRECTORY && (
                    item.type == MediaType.IMAGE ||
                    isLikelyImageByNameOrUri(item.title, item.uri)
                )
            }
            .sortedBy { img ->
                val imgStem = img.title.substringBeforeLast('.').lowercase().trim()
                when {
                    imgStem == videoStem -> 0
                    imgStem.contains("poster") || imgStem.contains("cover") || imgStem == "folder" -> 1
                    else -> 2
                }
            }

        for (img in images) {
            if (isVerticalImage(img.uri, img.resolution)) {
                return img.uri
            }
        }

        // Also check sub-folder/container albumArt if present
        for (item in folderItems) {
            val itemArt = item.thumbnailUrl
            if (!itemArt.isNullOrBlank() && isVerticalImage(itemArt, null)) {
                return itemArt
            }
        }
        return null
    }

    private suspend fun findPosterAcrossUpnpServer(
        controlUrl: String,
        targetItem: MediaItem,
        folderTitle: String?
    ): String? {
        val cds = ContentDirectoryService()
        val folderName = folderTitle?.trim()?.ifBlank { null }
        val currentParentId = targetItem.parentId
        val showQuery = extractCleanMediaTitle(folderName ?: targetItem.title)
        val stemTokens = showQuery.lowercase().split(' ').filter { it.length >= 3 }

        fun matchesTargetFolder(candidateTitle: String): Boolean {
            if (folderName != null && candidateTitle.equals(folderName, ignoreCase = true)) return true
            if (stemTokens.isNotEmpty()) {
                val lower = candidateTitle.lowercase()
                if (stemTokens.all { lower.contains(it) }) return true
            }
            return false
        }

        // 3a. Try UPnP Search for containers matching folderTitle or show name (catches clone under Pictures/Изображения)
        val containerQueries = buildList {
            if (folderName != null) {
                val esc = folderName.replace("\"", "\\\"")
                add("upnp:class derivedfrom \"object.container\" and dc:title = \"$esc\"")
                add("upnp:class derivedfrom \"object.container\" and dc:title contains \"$esc\"")
            }
            if (showQuery.length >= 3 && !showQuery.equals(folderName, ignoreCase = true)) {
                val escShow = showQuery.replace("\"", "\\\"")
                add("upnp:class derivedfrom \"object.container\" and dc:title contains \"$escShow\"")
            }
        }
        if (containerQueries.isNotEmpty()) {
            val matchingContainers = containerQueries
                .flatMap { q -> cds.search(controlUrl, "0", q, 20) }
                .distinctBy { it.id }

            for (container in matchingContainers) {
                if (container.id == currentParentId) continue
                val containerArt = container.thumbnailUrl
                if (!containerArt.isNullOrBlank() && isVerticalImage(containerArt, null)) {
                    return containerArt
                }
                val children = cds.browse(controlUrl, container.id)
                findVerticalPosterInList(targetItem, children)?.let { return it }
            }
        }

        // 3b. Traverse Pictures/Photos/Folders root branches if UPnP Search is unavailable or returned empty
        if (folderName != null || stemTokens.isNotEmpty()) {
            val rootItems = cds.browse(controlUrl, "0")
            val queue = ArrayDeque<Pair<String, Int>>()
            val visited = HashSet<String>()
            visited.add("0")
            currentParentId?.let { visited.add(it) }

            val priorityDirs = rootItems
                .filter { it.type == MediaType.DIRECTORY }
                .sortedBy { dir ->
                    val t = dir.title.lowercase()
                    when {
                        t.contains("изображ") || t.contains("фото") || t.contains("картин") ||
                        t.contains("picture") || t.contains("photo") || t.contains("image") -> 0
                        t.contains("папк") || t.contains("folder") || t.contains("all") || t.contains("все") -> 1
                        t.contains("music") || t.contains("музык") || t.contains("audio") || t.contains("аудио") ||
                        t.contains("video") || t.contains("видео") -> 9
                        else -> 3
                    }
                }

            for (dir in priorityDirs) {
                val t = dir.title.lowercase()
                val isSkippedCategory = t.contains("music") || t.contains("музык") || t.contains("audio") || t.contains("аудио")
                if (!isSkippedCategory && dir.id.isNotBlank() && visited.add(dir.id)) {
                    if (matchesTargetFolder(dir.title) && dir.id != currentParentId) {
                        val children = cds.browse(controlUrl, dir.id)
                        findVerticalPosterInList(targetItem, children)?.let { return it }
                    }
                    queue.addLast(dir.id to 1)
                }
            }

            var browseCalls = 0
            while (queue.isNotEmpty() && browseCalls < 35) {
                val (dirId, depth) = queue.removeFirst()
                browseCalls++
                val children = cds.browse(controlUrl, dirId)
                for (child in children) {
                    if (child.type != MediaType.DIRECTORY) continue
                    if (matchesTargetFolder(child.title) && child.id != currentParentId) {
                        val art = child.thumbnailUrl
                        if (!art.isNullOrBlank() && isVerticalImage(art, null)) return art
                        val matchItems = cds.browse(controlUrl, child.id)
                        findVerticalPosterInList(targetItem, matchItems)?.let { return it }
                    }
                    if (depth < 4 && child.id.isNotBlank() && visited.add(child.id)) {
                        val lt = child.title.lowercase()
                        val skipSub = lt == "all" || lt == "все" || lt.contains("recent") || lt.contains("недавн")
                        if (!skipSub) {
                            queue.addLast(child.id to (depth + 1))
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        // 3c. Flat UPnP Search for imageItem matching show/video stem
        if (stemTokens.isNotEmpty()) {
            val foundImgs = cds.search(controlUrl, "0", "upnp:class derivedfrom \"object.item.imageItem\"", 100)
            for (img in foundImgs) {
                val tLower = img.title.lowercase()
                val matchesFolderOrTitle = (currentParentId != null && img.parentId == currentParentId) ||
                    stemTokens.all { tLower.contains(it) }
                if (matchesFolderOrTitle && img.uri.isNotBlank() && isVerticalImage(img.uri, img.resolution)) {
                    return img.uri
                }
            }
        }

        return null
    }

    private fun extractCleanMediaTitle(rawName: String): String {
        var s = rawName.trim()
        // Strip file extension if present
        s = s.replace(Regex("\\.(avi|mkv|mp4|mov|ts|m2ts|webm|wmv|flv|mpeg|mpg|m4v)$", RegexOption.IGNORE_CASE), "")
        // Normalize separators
        s = s.replace('.', ' ').replace('_', ' ')
        // Cut at season/episode, year, or release tags
        s = s.replace(Regex("\\s+(?:S\\d{1,2}(?:E\\d{1,3})?|\\d{1,2}x\\d{1,2}|Season\\s*\\d+|Сезон\\s*\\d+|(?:19|20)\\d{2}|WEB[- ]?DL(?:Rip)?|HDRip|BDRip|BluRay|DVDRip|HDTV|1080p|720p|2160p|4K|x264|x265|HEVC|H\\.?264|H\\.?265|XviD|AC3|DTS|RUS|ENG|LF|RGzsRutracker).*$", RegexOption.IGNORE_CASE), "")
        s = s.replace(Regex("\\[.*?\\]|\\(.*?\\)"), " ")
        return s.replace(Regex("\\s+"), " ").trim()
    }

    private fun findOnlineVerticalPoster(folderTitle: String?, fileTitle: String): String? {
        val candidates = listOfNotNull(folderTitle, fileTitle)
            .map { extractCleanMediaTitle(it) }
            .filter { it.length >= 2 }
            .distinct()

        for (query in candidates) {
            // 1. TVMaze (great for TV series like "The Pitt")
            try {
                val encoded = URLEncoder.encode(query, "UTF-8")
                val req = Request.Builder()
                    .url("https://api.tvmaze.com/singlesearch/shows?q=$encoded")
                    .header("User-Agent", "RePlayer/1.0 (Android TV)")
                    .build()
                httpClient.newCall(req).execute().use { resp ->
                    if (resp.isSuccessful) {
                        val body = resp.body?.string()
                        if (!body.isNullOrBlank()) {
                            val jsonObj = JSONObject(body)
                            val imgObj = jsonObj.optJSONObject("image")
                            val posterUrl = imgObj?.optString("original")?.ifBlank { null }
                                ?: imgObj?.optString("medium")?.ifBlank { null }
                            if (!posterUrl.isNullOrBlank() && isVerticalImage(posterUrl, null)) {
                                return posterUrl
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            } catch (_: Throwable) {}

            // 2. iTunes Search API (works for both movies and TV shows, including Russian titles)
            val hasCyrillic = query.any { it in '\u0400'..'\u04FF' }
            val countryParam = if (hasCyrillic) "&country=ru&lang=ru_ru" else ""
            val mediaVariants = listOf("tvShow&entity=tvSeason", "movie&entity=movie")
            for (mediaType in mediaVariants) {
                try {
                    val encoded = URLEncoder.encode(query, "UTF-8")
                    val req = Request.Builder()
                        .url("https://itunes.apple.com/search?term=$encoded&media=$mediaType$countryParam&limit=3")
                        .header("User-Agent", "RePlayer/1.0 (Android TV)")
                        .build()
                    httpClient.newCall(req).execute().use { resp ->
                        if (resp.isSuccessful) {
                            val body = resp.body?.string()
                            if (!body.isNullOrBlank()) {
                                val results = JSONObject(body).optJSONArray("results")
                                if (results != null) {
                                    for (i in 0 until results.length()) {
                                        val art100 = results.getJSONObject(i).optString("artworkUrl100").ifBlank { null } ?: continue
                                        val hiRes = art100.replace("100x100bb", "600x600bb")
                                        if (isVerticalImage(hiRes, null)) {
                                            return hiRes
                                        }
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                } catch (_: Throwable) {}
            }
        }
        return null
    }

    private fun isLikelyImageByNameOrUri(title: String, uri: String): Boolean {
        val t = title.lowercase()
        val u = uri.substringBefore('?').substringBefore('#').lowercase()
        return listOf(".jpg", ".jpeg", ".png", ".webp", ".bmp", ".gif").any {
            t.endsWith(it) || u.endsWith(it)
        }
    }

    private fun isVerticalImage(url: String, resolutionHint: String?): Boolean {
        if (!resolutionHint.isNullOrBlank()) {
            val parts = resolutionHint.lowercase().split('x')
            if (parts.size == 2) {
                val w = parts[0].trim().toIntOrNull()
                val h = parts[1].trim().toIntOrNull()
                if (w != null && h != null && w > 0 && h > 0) {
                    return h > w
                }
            }
        }

        return try {
            val req = Request.Builder()
                .url(url)
                .header("User-Agent", "RePlayer/1.0 (Android TV)")
                .build()
            httpClient.newCall(req).execute().use { resp ->
                if (!resp.isSuccessful) return false
                val stream = resp.body?.byteStream() ?: return false
                val maxHeaderBytes = 262144
                val buffer = ByteArray(maxHeaderBytes)
                var totalRead = 0
                while (totalRead < maxHeaderBytes) {
                    val read = stream.read(buffer, totalRead, maxHeaderBytes - totalRead)
                    if (read <= 0) break
                    totalRead += read

                    // Direct PNG IHDR header check (first 24 bytes: width at 16..19, height at 20..23)
                    if (totalRead >= 24 && isPngSignature(buffer)) {
                        val w = readIntBE(buffer, 16)
                        val h = readIntBE(buffer, 20)
                        if (w > 0 && h > 0) return h > w
                    }

                    // Direct JPEG / WEBP / GIF bounds check via BitmapFactory once enough bytes buffered
                    if (totalRead >= 16384) {
                        val quickOpts = BitmapFactory.Options().apply { inJustDecodeBounds = true }
                        BitmapFactory.decodeByteArray(buffer, 0, totalRead, quickOpts)
                        if (quickOpts.outWidth > 0 && quickOpts.outHeight > 0) {
                            return quickOpts.outHeight > quickOpts.outWidth
                        }
                    }
                }
                if (totalRead <= 0) return false
                if (totalRead >= 24 && isPngSignature(buffer)) {
                    val w = readIntBE(buffer, 16)
                    val h = readIntBE(buffer, 20)
                    if (w > 0 && h > 0) return h > w
                }
                val opts = BitmapFactory.Options().apply { inJustDecodeBounds = true }
                BitmapFactory.decodeByteArray(buffer, 0, totalRead, opts)
                opts.outWidth > 0 && opts.outHeight > opts.outWidth
            }
        } catch (_: Throwable) {
            false
        }
    }

    private fun isPngSignature(bytes: ByteArray): Boolean {
        return bytes.size >= 24 &&
            bytes[0] == 0x89.toByte() &&
            bytes[1] == 0x50.toByte() &&
            bytes[2] == 0x4E.toByte() &&
            bytes[3] == 0x47.toByte() &&
            bytes[4] == 0x0D.toByte() &&
            bytes[5] == 0x0A.toByte() &&
            bytes[6] == 0x1A.toByte() &&
            bytes[7] == 0x0A.toByte() &&
            bytes[12] == 0x49.toByte() && // 'I'
            bytes[13] == 0x48.toByte() && // 'H'
            bytes[14] == 0x44.toByte() && // 'D'
            bytes[15] == 0x52.toByte()    // 'R'
    }

    private fun readIntBE(bytes: ByteArray, offset: Int): Int {
        return ((bytes[offset].toInt() and 0xFF) shl 24) or
            ((bytes[offset + 1].toInt() and 0xFF) shl 16) or
            ((bytes[offset + 2].toInt() and 0xFF) shl 8) or
            (bytes[offset + 3].toInt() and 0xFF)
    }

    fun updateProgress(id: String, title: String, uri: String, positionMs: Long, durationMs: Long) {
        if (durationMs <= 0L || positionMs < 0L) return

        val entry = MediaWatchProgress(
            positionMs = positionMs,
            durationMs = durationMs,
            updatedAt = System.currentTimeMillis()
        )
        val updatedMap = _progressMap.value.toMutableMap()
        updatedMap[id] = entry
        if (uri.isNotBlank()) updatedMap[uri] = entry
        saveProgressMap(updatedMap)

        val currentRecent = _recentList.value.toMutableList()
        val idx = currentRecent.indexOfFirst { it.id == id || it.uri == uri }
        if (idx >= 0) {
            currentRecent[idx] = currentRecent[idx].copy(
                positionMs = positionMs,
                durationMs = durationMs,
                timestamp = System.currentTimeMillis()
            )
            saveRecent(currentRecent)
        }
    }

    fun getSavedResumePosition(id: String, uri: String? = null): Long {
        val entry = _progressMap.value[id] ?: uri?.let { _progressMap.value[it] } ?: return 0L
        if (entry.durationMs <= 10_000L || entry.positionMs < 5_000L) return 0L
        val frac = entry.progress()
        return if (frac in 0.01f..0.95f) entry.positionMs else 0L
    }

    fun removeRecent(id: String) {
        val current = _recentList.value.filterNot { it.id == id }
        saveRecent(current)
    }
}

package com.replayer.data

import android.content.Context
import android.content.SharedPreferences
import com.replayer.model.UpnpDevice
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.asStateFlow
import kotlinx.serialization.encodeToString
import kotlinx.serialization.json.Json

class ServerConfigManager(context: Context) {

    private val prefs: SharedPreferences =
        context.getSharedPreferences("replayer_server_config", Context.MODE_PRIVATE)

    private val json = Json { ignoreUnknownKeys = true }

    private val _connectedUpnpDevice = MutableStateFlow<UpnpDevice?>(loadConnectedDevice())
    val connectedUpnpDevice: StateFlow<UpnpDevice?> = _connectedUpnpDevice.asStateFlow()

    private fun loadConnectedDevice(): UpnpDevice? {
        val raw = prefs.getString("connected_upnp_device", null) ?: return null
        return try {
            json.decodeFromString<UpnpDevice>(raw)
        } catch (_: Exception) {
            null
        }
    }

    fun connectUpnpDevice(device: UpnpDevice) {
        _connectedUpnpDevice.value = device
        try {
            val encoded = json.encodeToString(device)
            prefs.edit().putString("connected_upnp_device", encoded).apply()
        } catch (_: Exception) {}
    }

    fun disconnectUpnpDevice() {
        _connectedUpnpDevice.value = null
        prefs.edit().remove("connected_upnp_device").apply()
    }
}

[ФАЙЛ 21: app/src/main/java/com/replayer/diagnostics/CrashReporter.kt] (733 lines)

package com.replayer.diagnostics

import android.app.ActivityManager
import android.app.ApplicationExitInfo
import android.content.Context
import android.content.SharedPreferences
import android.os.Build
import android.os.Process
import android.util.Log
import com.replayer.BuildConfig
import kotlinx.coroutines.CoroutineScope
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.SupervisorJob
import kotlinx.coroutines.delay
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.asStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext
import okhttp3.FormBody
import okhttp3.MediaType.Companion.toMediaType
import okhttp3.MultipartBody
import okhttp3.OkHttpClient
import okhttp3.Request
import okhttp3.RequestBody.Companion.toRequestBody
import org.json.JSONObject
import java.io.File
import java.text.SimpleDateFormat
import java.util.Date
import java.util.Locale
import java.util.concurrent.Executors
import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicBoolean

data class CrashReportInfo(
    val fileName: String,
    val title: String,
    val timestampMs: Long,
    val isSent: Boolean,
    val preview: String,
    val fullText: String
)

object CrashReporter {
    private const val PREFS_NAME = "replayer_diagnostics"
    private const val KEY_BOT_TOKEN = "tg_bot_token"
    private const val KEY_CHAT_ID = "tg_chat_id"
    private const val KEY_AUTO_SEND = "tg_auto_send"
    private const val KEY_LAST_EXIT_TS = "last_exit_check_ts"
    private const val MAX_BREADCRUMBS = 80
    private const val MAX_REPORTS_KEPT = 12

    private val initialized = AtomicBoolean(false)
    private val handlingCrash = AtomicBoolean(false)

    private lateinit var appContext: Context
    private lateinit var prefs: SharedPreferences
    private lateinit var reportsDir: File
    private lateinit var currentBreadcrumbsFile: File
    private lateinit var prevBreadcrumbsFile: File

    private var previousHandler: Thread.UncaughtExceptionHandler? = null

    private val breadcrumbLock = Any()
    private val breadcrumbs = ArrayDeque<String>(MAX_BREADCRUMBS)
    private val diskExecutor = Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor()
    private val scope = CoroutineScope(SupervisorJob() + Dispatchers.IO)

    private val httpClient = OkHttpClient.Builder()
        .connectTimeout(15, TimeUnit.SECONDS)
        .readTimeout(25, TimeUnit.SECONDS)
        .writeTimeout(25, TimeUnit.SECONDS)
        .build()

    private val _reports = MutableStateFlow<List<CrashReportInfo>>(emptyList())
    val reports: StateFlow<List<CrashReportInfo>> = _reports.asStateFlow()

    private val _lastSendStatus = MutableStateFlow<String?>(null)
    val lastSendStatus: StateFlow<String?> = _lastSendStatus.asStateFlow()

    fun init(context: Context) {
        if (!initialized.compareAndSet(false, true)) return
        appContext = context.applicationContext
        prefs = appContext.getSharedPreferences(PREFS_NAME, Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
        reportsDir = File(appContext.filesDir, "crash_reports").apply { mkdirs() }
        val pid = Process.myPid()
        currentBreadcrumbsFile = File(reportsDir, "breadcrumbs_${pid}.log")
        prevBreadcrumbsFile = File(reportsDir, "prev_session_breadcrumbs.log")

        // Prune stale per-PID breadcrumb files (keep up to 6 newest within last 48h)
        try {
            val cutoff = System.currentTimeMillis() - 48L * 3600 * 1000L
            val pidLogs = reportsDir.listFiles { f ->
                f.isFile && f.name.startsWith("breadcrumbs_") && f.name.endsWith(".log")
            }?.sortedByDescending { it.lastModified() } ?: emptyList()
            pidLogs.forEachIndexed { idx, file ->
                if (idx >= 6 || file.lastModified() < cutoff) {
                    file.delete()
                }
            }
            if (currentBreadcrumbsFile.exists()) {
                currentBreadcrumbsFile.writeText("")
            }
        } catch (_: Throwable) {}

        previousHandler = Thread.getDefaultUncaughtExceptionHandler()
        Thread.setDefaultUncaughtExceptionHandler { thread, throwable ->
            handleUncaughtException(thread, throwable)
        }

        breadcrumb("App", "Start Re:Player v${BuildConfig.VERSION_NAME} (${BuildConfig.VERSION_CODE}) on ${Build.MANUFACTURER} ${Build.MODEL} Android ${Build.VERSION.RELEASE}")

        refreshReportsList()

        // Inspect historical OS exit reasons (Android 11+) and send pending crash reports in background
        scope.launch {
            checkHistoricalProcessExits()
            refreshReportsList()
            delay(3500)
            if (isAutoSendEnabled() && isConfigured()) {
                sendPendingReportsInternal()
            }
        }
    }

    fun hasBuiltInConfig(): Boolean {
        return BuildConfig.DEFAULT_TG_BOT_TOKEN.trim().isNotBlank() &&
            BuildConfig.DEFAULT_TG_CHAT_ID.trim().isNotBlank()
    }

    fun hasCustomOverride(): Boolean {
        if (!::prefs.isInitialized) return false
        return !prefs.getString(KEY_BOT_TOKEN, null).isNullOrBlank()
    }

    fun getCustomBotToken(): String {
        if (!::prefs.isInitialized) return ""
        return prefs.getString(KEY_BOT_TOKEN, null)?.trim().orEmpty()
    }

    fun getCustomChatId(): String {
        if (!::prefs.isInitialized) return ""
        return prefs.getString(KEY_CHAT_ID, null)?.trim().orEmpty()
    }

    fun getBotToken(): String {
        if (!::prefs.isInitialized) return BuildConfig.DEFAULT_TG_BOT_TOKEN.trim()
        val saved = prefs.getString(KEY_BOT_TOKEN, null)?.trim()
        return if (!saved.isNullOrEmpty()) saved else BuildConfig.DEFAULT_TG_BOT_TOKEN.trim()
    }

    fun getChatId(): String {
        if (!::prefs.isInitialized) return BuildConfig.DEFAULT_TG_CHAT_ID.trim()
        val saved = prefs.getString(KEY_CHAT_ID, null)?.trim()
        return if (!saved.isNullOrEmpty()) saved else BuildConfig.DEFAULT_TG_CHAT_ID.trim()
    }

    fun clearCustomConfig() {
        if (!::prefs.isInitialized) return
        prefs.edit()
            .remove(KEY_BOT_TOKEN)
            .remove(KEY_CHAT_ID)
            .commit()
    }

    fun isConfigured(): Boolean {
        return getBotToken().isNotBlank() && getChatId().isNotBlank()
    }

    fun isAutoSendEnabled(): Boolean {
        if (!::prefs.isInitialized) return true
        return prefs.getBoolean(KEY_AUTO_SEND, true)
    }

    fun setAutoSendEnabled(enabled: Boolean) {
        if (!::prefs.isInitialized) return
        prefs.edit().putBoolean(KEY_AUTO_SEND, enabled).apply()
    }

    fun saveTelegramConfig(botToken: String, chatId: String) {
        if (!::prefs.isInitialized) return
        prefs.edit()
            .putString(KEY_BOT_TOKEN, botToken.trim())
            .putString(KEY_CHAT_ID, chatId.trim())
            .commit()
    }

    fun breadcrumb(tag: String, message: String) {
        if (!initialized.get()) return
        val ts = SimpleDateFormat("HH:mm:ss.SSS", Locale.US).format(Date())
        val line = "$ts [$tag] ${message.replace('\n', ' ').take(300)}"
        val snapshotText: String
        synchronized(breadcrumbLock) {
            if (breadcrumbs.size >= MAX_BREADCRUMBS) {
                breadcrumbs.removeFirst()
            }
            breadcrumbs.addLast(line)
            snapshotText = breadcrumbs.joinToString("\n")
        }
        try {
            diskExecutor.execute {
                try {
                    currentBreadcrumbsFile.writeText(snapshotText)
                } catch (_: Throwable) {}
            }
        } catch (_: Throwable) {}
    }

    private fun handleUncaughtException(thread: Thread, throwable: Throwable) {
        if (handlingCrash.compareAndSet(false, true)) {
            try {
                val now = System.currentTimeMillis()
                val report = buildReportText(
                    kind = "UNCAUGHT EXCEPTION",
                    timestampMs = now,
                    threadName = thread.name,
                    throwable = throwable,
                    extraDetails = null,
                    breadcrumbsOverride = null,
                    includeLogcat = true
                )
                writePendingReport(now, "crash", report)
            } catch (_: Throwable) {
            }
        }
        try {
            previousHandler?.uncaughtException(thread, throwable)
        } catch (_: Throwable) {
            Process.killProcess(Process.myPid())
        }
    }

    private fun checkHistoricalProcessExits() {
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT < Build.VERSION_CODES.R) return
        try {
            val am = appContext.getSystemService(Context.ACTIVITY_SERVICE) as? ActivityManager ?: return
            val reasons = am.getHistoricalProcessExitReasons(appContext.packageName, 0, 5)
            if (reasons.isEmpty()) return

            val lastCheckedTs = prefs.getLong(KEY_LAST_EXIT_TS, 0L)
            var newestSeenTs = lastCheckedTs

            // Process oldest-to-newest so chronological order stays clean
            for (info in reasons.reversed()) {
                val ts = info.timestamp
                if (ts <= lastCheckedTs) continue
                if (ts > newestSeenTs) newestSeenTs = ts

                val reasonCode = info.reason
                val isUnexpected = reasonCode == ApplicationExitInfo.REASON_CRASH_NATIVE ||
                    reasonCode == ApplicationExitInfo.REASON_LOW_MEMORY ||
                    reasonCode == ApplicationExitInfo.REASON_ANR ||
                    reasonCode == ApplicationExitInfo.REASON_CRASH ||
                    reasonCode == ApplicationExitInfo.REASON_EXCESSIVE_RESOURCE_USAGE

                if (!isUnexpected) continue

                // Avoid duplicate report if UncaughtExceptionHandler already wrote one within +-15s
                val alreadyHaveJavaCrashNearTs = reportsDir.listFiles()?.any { f ->
                    val fTs = extractTimestampFromName(f.name)
                    fTs > 0L && kotlin.math.abs(fTs - ts) < 15_000L
                } == true

                if (reasonCode == ApplicationExitInfo.REASON_CRASH && alreadyHaveJavaCrashNearTs) {
                    continue
                }

                val reasonLabel = when (reasonCode) {
                    ApplicationExitInfo.REASON_CRASH_NATIVE -> "NATIVE CRASH (LibVLC / C++)"
                    ApplicationExitInfo.REASON_LOW_MEMORY -> "OUT OF MEMORY (Killed by Android OS)"
                    ApplicationExitInfo.REASON_ANR -> "ANR (UI Freeze)"
                    ApplicationExitInfo.REASON_EXCESSIVE_RESOURCE_USAGE -> "EXCESSIVE RESOURCE USAGE"
                    ApplicationExitInfo.REASON_CRASH -> "PROCESS CRASH"
                    else -> "EXIT REASON #$reasonCode"
                }

                val traceSnippet = try {
                    info.traceInputStream?.bufferedReader(Charsets.UTF_8)?.use { reader ->
                        val lines = mutableListOf<String>()
                        var count = 0
                        while (count < 250) {
                            val line = reader.readLine() ?: break
                            lines.add(line)
                            count++
                        }
                        lines.joinToString("\n").takeIf { it.isNotBlank() }
                    }
                } catch (_: Throwable) {
                    null
                }

                val details = buildString {
                    appendLine("Exit Reason: $reasonLabel ($reasonCode)")
                    appendLine("PID: ${info.pid} | Status: ${info.status} | Importance: ${info.importance}")
                    appendLine("PSS: ${info.pss} KB | RSS: ${info.rss} KB")
                    if (!info.description.isNullOrBlank()) {
                        appendLine("Description: ${info.description}")
                    }
                    if (!traceSnippet.isNullOrBlank()) {
                        appendLine("\n--- OS Trace ---")
                        appendLine(traceSnippet)
                    }
                }

                val pidCrumbsFile = File(reportsDir, "breadcrumbs_${info.pid}.log")
                val prevCrumbs = try {
                    when {
                        pidCrumbsFile.exists() -> pidCrumbsFile.readText()
                        prevBreadcrumbsFile.exists() -> prevBreadcrumbsFile.readText()
                        else -> null
                    }
                } catch (_: Throwable) {
                    null
                }

                val report = buildReportText(
                    kind = reasonLabel,
                    timestampMs = ts,
                    threadName = info.processName,
                    throwable = null,
                    extraDetails = details,
                    breadcrumbsOverride = prevCrumbs,
                    includeLogcat = false
                )
                writePendingReport(ts, "os_exit", report)
            }

            if (newestSeenTs > lastCheckedTs) {
                prefs.edit().putLong(KEY_LAST_EXIT_TS, newestSeenTs).apply()
            }
        } catch (_: Throwable) {}
    }

    private fun buildReportText(
        kind: String,
        timestampMs: Long,
        threadName: String?,
        throwable: Throwable?,
        extraDetails: String?,
        breadcrumbsOverride: String?,
        includeLogcat: Boolean
    ): String {
        val dateStr = SimpleDateFormat("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss z", Locale.US).format(Date(timestampMs))
        val rt = Runtime.getRuntime()
        val usedMb = (rt.totalMemory() - rt.freeMemory()) / (1024 * 1024)
        val maxMb = rt.maxMemory() / (1024 * 1024)

        val memInfo = try {
            val am = appContext.getSystemService(Context.ACTIVITY_SERVICE) as? ActivityManager
            ActivityManager.MemoryInfo().also { am?.getMemoryInfo(it) }
        } catch (_: Throwable) {
            null
        }

        val crumbText = breadcrumbsOverride ?: synchronized(breadcrumbLock) {
            breadcrumbs.joinToString("\n")
        }

        val logcatText = if (includeLogcat) readOwnLogcat(250) else ""

        return buildString {
            appendLine("=== RE:PLAYER CRASH REPORT ===")
            appendLine("Type: $kind")
            appendLine("Time: $dateStr")
            appendLine("App: ${BuildConfig.VERSION_NAME} (code ${BuildConfig.VERSION_CODE})")
            appendLine("Device: ${Build.MANUFACTURER} ${Build.MODEL} (${Build.DEVICE})")
            appendLine("Android: ${Build.VERSION.RELEASE} (SDK ${Build.VERSION.SDK_INT})")
            appendLine("ABI: ${Build.SUPPORTED_ABIS.joinToString(", ")}")
            if (!threadName.isNullOrBlank()) appendLine("Thread: $threadName")
            appendLine("JVM Heap: ${usedMb}MB / ${maxMb}MB")
            if (memInfo != null) {
                val availMb = memInfo.availMem / (1024 * 1024)
                val totMb = memInfo.totalMem / (1024 * 1024)
                appendLine("System RAM: ${availMb}MB free / ${totMb}MB total (lowMemory=${memInfo.lowMemory})")
            }

            if (throwable != null) {
                appendLine("\n--- Stacktrace ---")
                appendLine(Log.getStackTraceString(throwable).trim())
            }

            if (!extraDetails.isNullOrBlank()) {
                appendLine("\n--- Details ---")
                appendLine(extraDetails.trim())
            }

            if (crumbText.isNotBlank()) {
                appendLine("\n--- Last Actions (Breadcrumbs) ---")
                appendLine(crumbText.trim())
            }

            if (logcatText.isNotBlank()) {
                appendLine("\n--- Logcat ---")
                appendLine(logcatText.trim())
            }
        }
    }

    private fun readOwnLogcat(lines: Int): String {
        return try {
            val pid = Process.myPid()
            val cmd = if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.N) {
                arrayOf("logcat", "-d", "-t", lines.toString(), "--pid=$pid", "-v", "threadtime")
            } else {
                arrayOf("logcat", "-d", "-t", lines.toString(), "-v", "threadtime")
            }
            val proc = Runtime.getRuntime().exec(cmd)
            val text = proc.inputStream.bufferedReader(Charsets.UTF_8).use { it.readText() }
            try { proc.destroy() } catch (_: Throwable) {}
            text.takeLast(32000)
        } catch (_: Throwable) {
            ""
        }
    }

    private fun writePendingReport(timestampMs: Long, prefix: String, content: String): File {
        val file = File(reportsDir, "${prefix}_${timestampMs}.pending.txt")
        file.writeText(content, Charsets.UTF_8)
        pruneOldReports()
        refreshReportsList()
        return file
    }

    private fun pruneOldReports() {
        try {
            val files = reportsDir.listFiles { f ->
                f.isFile && (f.name.endsWith(".pending.txt") || f.name.endsWith(".sent.txt"))
            }?.sortedByDescending { extractTimestampFromName(it.name) } ?: return
            if (files.size > MAX_REPORTS_KEPT) {
                files.drop(MAX_REPORTS_KEPT).forEach { it.delete() }
            }
        } catch (_: Throwable) {}
    }

    private fun extractTimestampFromName(name: String): Long {
        val digits = name.substringAfter('_').substringBefore('.')
        return digits.toLongOrNull() ?: 0L
    }

    fun refreshReportsList() {
        if (!::reportsDir.isInitialized) return
        try {
            val files = reportsDir.listFiles { f ->
                f.isFile && (f.name.endsWith(".pending.txt") || f.name.endsWith(".sent.txt"))
            }?.sortedByDescending { extractTimestampFromName(it.name) } ?: emptyList()

            _reports.value = files.map { file ->
                val text = try { file.readText(Charsets.UTF_8) } catch (_: Throwable) { "" }
                val lines = text.lines()
                val typeLine = lines.firstOrNull { it.startsWith("Type:") }?.removePrefix("Type:")?.trim() ?: "Crash Report"
                val previewLine = lines.firstOrNull {
                    it.contains("Exception") || it.contains("Error") || it.startsWith("Exit Reason:")
                }?.trim() ?: lines.drop(1).firstOrNull { it.isNotBlank() }?.trim() ?: ""

                CrashReportInfo(
                    fileName = file.name,
                    title = typeLine,
                    timestampMs = extractTimestampFromName(file.name).takeIf { it > 0L } ?: file.lastModified(),
                    isSent = file.name.endsWith(".sent.txt"),
                    preview = previewLine.take(140),
                    fullText = text
                )
            }
        } catch (_: Throwable) {}
    }

    fun getReportFile(fileName: String): File? {
        if (!::reportsDir.isInitialized) return null
        val safeName = File(fileName).name
        val target = File(reportsDir, safeName)
        return if (target.exists() && target.isFile) target else null
    }

    fun clearReports() {
        if (!::reportsDir.isInitialized) return
        try {
            reportsDir.listFiles { f ->
                f.isFile && (f.name.endsWith(".pending.txt") || f.name.endsWith(".sent.txt"))
            }?.forEach { it.delete() }
            refreshReportsList()
        } catch (_: Throwable) {}
    }

    suspend fun detectChatIdFromBot(botToken: String): Result<Pair<String, String>> = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        val cleanToken = botToken.trim()
        if (cleanToken.isBlank() || !cleanToken.contains(':')) {
            return@withContext Result.failure(IllegalArgumentException("Неверный формат токена бота"))
        }
        try {
            val req = Request.Builder()
                .url("https://api.telegram.org/bot$cleanToken/getUpdates?limit=20")
                .build()
            httpClient.newCall(req).execute().use { resp ->
                val body = resp.body?.string().orEmpty()
                if (!resp.isSuccessful) {
                    val desc = try { JSONObject(body).optString("description") } catch (_: Throwable) { "" }
                    return@withContext Result.failure(IllegalStateException(desc.ifBlank { "HTTP ${resp.code}" }))
                }
                val root = JSONObject(body)
                if (!root.optBoolean("ok", false)) {
                    return@withContext Result.failure(IllegalStateException(root.optString("description", "Ошибка Telegram API")))
                }
                val updates = root.optJSONArray("result")
                if (updates == null || updates.length() == 0) {
                    return@withContext Result.failure(IllegalStateException("Сообщений боту ещё нет. Напишите боту /start в Telegram и повторите."))
                }
                // Pick the newest update that contains a chat object
                for (i in updates.length() - 1 downTo 0) {
                    val upd = updates.getJSONObject(i)
                    val msg = upd.optJSONObject("message")
                        ?: upd.optJSONObject("edited_message")
                        ?: upd.optJSONObject("channel_post")
                        ?: upd.optJSONObject("my_chat_member")
                    val chat = msg?.optJSONObject("chat") ?: continue
                    val id = chat.optLong("id", 0L)
                    if (id != 0L) {
                        val title = chat.optString("title").ifBlank {
                            listOfNotNull(
                                chat.optString("first_name").ifBlank { null },
                                chat.optString("last_name").ifBlank { null }
                            ).joinToString(" ").ifBlank {
                                chat.optString("username").ifBlank { "Chat $id" }
                            }
                        }
                        return@withContext Result.success(id.toString() to title)
                    }
                }
                Result.failure(IllegalStateException("Не удалось найти chat_id. Напишите боту /start в Telegram и повторите."))
            }
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Result.failure(e)
        }
    }

    suspend fun sendTestMessage(): Result<String> = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        val token = getBotToken()
        val chatId = getChatId()
        if (token.isBlank() || chatId.isBlank()) {
            return@withContext Result.failure(IllegalStateException("Сначала укажите Bot Token и Chat ID"))
        }
        val msg = "Re:Player v${BuildConfig.VERSION_NAME} (${BuildConfig.VERSION_CODE}) подключён к Telegram.\n" +
            "Устройство: ${Build.MANUFACTURER} ${Build.MODEL} (Android ${Build.VERSION.RELEASE}).\n" +
            "Автоматический сбор логов вылетов активен."
        val ok = sendTelegramText(token, chatId, msg)
        if (ok.isSuccess) {
            _lastSendStatus.value = "Тестовое сообщение отправлено в Telegram"
            // Also flush any pending crash reports
            val sentCount = sendPendingReportsInternal()
            if (sentCount > 0) {
                _lastSendStatus.value = "Подключено! Отправлено ожидающих отчётов: $sentCount"
            }
            Result.success(_lastSendStatus.value ?: "Отправлено")
        } else {
            val err = ok.exceptionOrNull()?.message ?: "Ошибка отправки"
            _lastSendStatus.value = "Ошибка: $err"
            Result.failure(IllegalStateException(err))
        }
    }

    suspend fun sendDiagnosticSnapshotNow(): Result<String> = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        val token = getBotToken()
        val chatId = getChatId()
        if (token.isBlank() || chatId.isBlank()) {
            return@withContext Result.failure(IllegalStateException("Сначала настройте Bot Token и Chat ID"))
        }
        val now = System.currentTimeMillis()
        val report = buildReportText(
            kind = "MANUAL DIAGNOSTIC SNAPSHOT",
            timestampMs = now,
            threadName = Thread.currentThread().name,
            throwable = null,
            extraDetails = "Manually triggered diagnostic snapshot from Settings.",
            breadcrumbsOverride = null,
            includeLogcat = true
        )
        val fileName = "replayer_diag_${now}.txt"
        val summary = buildSummaryCaption(report)
        val textRes = sendTelegramText(token, chatId, summary)
        val docRes = sendTelegramDocument(token, chatId, fileName, report, "Снимок диагностики Re:Player v${BuildConfig.VERSION_NAME}")
        if (docRes.isSuccess) {
            // Flush pending crash files too if any
            val flushed = sendPendingReportsInternal()
            val statusMsg = if (flushed > 0) {
                "Диагностика и $flushed отчёт(ов) о сбоях отправлены в Telegram"
            } else {
                "Диагностический лог отправлен в Telegram"
            }
            _lastSendStatus.value = statusMsg
            Result.success(statusMsg)
        } else {
            val err = docRes.exceptionOrNull()?.message ?: textRes.exceptionOrNull()?.message ?: "Сбой сети"
            _lastSendStatus.value = "Ошибка отправки: $err"
            Result.failure(IllegalStateException(err))
        }
    }

    suspend fun sendPendingReports(): Int = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        sendPendingReportsInternal()
    }

    private fun sendPendingReportsInternal(): Int {
        if (!::reportsDir.isInitialized || !isConfigured()) return 0
        val token = getBotToken()
        val chatId = getChatId()
        val pendingFiles = reportsDir.listFiles { f ->
            f.isFile && f.name.endsWith(".pending.txt")
        }?.sortedBy { extractTimestampFromName(it.name) } ?: return 0

        var sentCount = 0
        for (file in pendingFiles) {
            val fullText = try { file.readText(Charsets.UTF_8) } catch (_: Throwable) { "" }
            if (fullText.isBlank()) {
                file.delete()
                continue
            }
            val summary = buildSummaryCaption(fullText)
            val textRes = sendTelegramText(token, chatId, summary)
            val docRes = sendTelegramDocument(
                token = token,
                chatId = chatId,
                fileName = file.name.replace(".pending.txt", ".txt"),
                content = fullText,
                caption = "Полный лог сбоя Re:Player v${BuildConfig.VERSION_NAME}"
            )
            if (textRes.isSuccess || docRes.isSuccess) {
                val sentFile = File(reportsDir, file.name.replace(".pending.txt", ".sent.txt"))
                file.renameTo(sentFile)
                sentCount++
            }
        }
        if (sentCount > 0) {
            _lastSendStatus.value = "Отправлено отчётов в Telegram: $sentCount"
            refreshReportsList()
        }
        return sentCount
    }

    private fun buildSummaryCaption(fullReport: String): String {
        val lines = fullReport.lines()
        val header = lines.takeWhile { !it.startsWith("--- Logcat ---") && !it.startsWith("--- OS Trace ---") }
        // Keep device metadata + first ~22 lines of stacktrace/details + last 8 breadcrumbs
        val stackStart = header.indexOfFirst { it.startsWith("--- Stacktrace ---") || it.startsWith("--- Details ---") }
        val crumbsStart = header.indexOfFirst { it.startsWith("--- Last Actions") }

        val sb = StringBuilder()
        val metaEnd = if (stackStart >= 0) stackStart else header.size
        header.take(metaEnd).forEach { sb.appendLine(it) }

        if (stackStart >= 0) {
            val stackEnd = if (crumbsStart > stackStart) crumbsStart else header.size
            val stackSlice = header.subList(stackStart, minOf(stackEnd, stackStart + 25))
            sb.appendLine()
            stackSlice.forEach { sb.appendLine(it) }
        }

        if (crumbsStart >= 0) {
            val crumbLines = header.subList(crumbsStart + 1, header.size).filter { it.isNotBlank() }.takeLast(8)
            if (crumbLines.isNotEmpty()) {
                sb.appendLine("\n--- Последние действия ---")
                crumbLines.forEach { sb.appendLine(it) }
            }
        }

        return sb.toString().trim().take(3800)
    }

    private fun sendTelegramText(botToken: String, chatId: String, text: String): Result<Unit> {
        return try {
            val formBody = FormBody.Builder()
                .add("chat_id", chatId)
                .add("text", text.take(4000))
                .build()
            val req = Request.Builder()
                .url("https://api.telegram.org/bot$botToken/sendMessage")
                .post(formBody)
                .build()
            httpClient.newCall(req).execute().use { resp ->
                if (resp.isSuccessful) {
                    Result.success(Unit)
                } else if ((resp.code == 401 || resp.code == 404) && hasCustomOverride() && hasBuiltInConfig() && botToken == getCustomBotToken()) {
                    // Auto-recover if user's old saved token was revoked and built-in token exists
                    clearCustomConfig()
                    sendTelegramText(getBotToken(), getChatId(), text)
                } else {
                    val body = resp.body?.string().orEmpty()
                    val desc = try { JSONObject(body).optString("description") } catch (_: Throwable) { "" }
                    Result.failure(IllegalStateException(desc.ifBlank { "HTTP ${resp.code}" }))
                }
            }
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Result.failure(e)
        }
    }

    private fun sendTelegramDocument(
        token: String,
        chatId: String,
        fileName: String,
        content: String,
        caption: String
    ): Result<Unit> {
        return try {
            val body = MultipartBody.Builder()
                .setType(MultipartBody.FORM)
                .addFormDataPart("chat_id", chatId)
                .addFormDataPart("caption", caption.take(1000))
                .addFormDataPart(
                    "document",
                    fileName,
                    content.toRequestBody("text/plain; charset=utf-8".toMediaType())
                )
                .build()
            val req = Request.Builder()
                .url("https://api.telegram.org/bot$token/sendDocument")
                .post(body)
                .build()
            httpClient.newCall(req).execute().use { resp ->
                if (resp.isSuccessful) {
                    Result.success(Unit)
                } else if ((resp.code == 401 || resp.code == 404) && hasCustomOverride() && hasBuiltInConfig() && token == getCustomBotToken()) {
                    clearCustomConfig()
                    sendTelegramDocument(getBotToken(), getChatId(), fileName, content, caption)
                } else {
                    val respStr = resp.body?.string().orEmpty()
                    val desc = try { JSONObject(respStr).optString("description") } catch (_: Throwable) { "" }
                    Result.failure(IllegalStateException(desc.ifBlank { "HTTP ${resp.code}" }))
                }
            }
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Result.failure(e)
        }
    }
}

================================================================================
[ФАЙЛ 22: app/src/main/java/com/replayer/diagnostics/TelegramConfigServer.kt] (407 lines)
