package com.replayer.diagnostics

import kotlinx.coroutines.CoroutineScope
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.Job
import kotlinx.coroutines.isActive
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
import kotlinx.coroutines.runBlocking
import java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream
import java.io.InputStream
import java.net.Inet4Address
import java.net.NetworkInterface
import java.net.ServerSocket
import java.net.Socket
import java.net.URLDecoder
import java.net.URLEncoder
import java.text.SimpleDateFormat
import java.util.Date
import java.util.Locale

class TelegramConfigServer(
    private val onConfigUpdated: () -> Unit
) {
    private var serverSocket: ServerSocket? = null
    private var acceptJob: Job? = null
    private val scope = CoroutineScope(Dispatchers.IO)

    var port: Int = 8765
        private set

    fun start(): String? {
        stop()
        val ip = findLocalIpv4Address() ?: return null
        val portsToTry = listOf(8765, 8766, 8767, 8768, 8789)
        var boundSocket: ServerSocket? = null
        for (p in portsToTry) {
            try {
                boundSocket = ServerSocket(p).apply {
                    reuseAddress = true
                }
                port = p
                break
            } catch (_: Throwable) {}
        }
        if (boundSocket == null) return null
        serverSocket = boundSocket

        acceptJob = scope.launch {
            while (isActive && !boundSocket.isClosed) {
                try {
                    val client = boundSocket.accept()
                    launch { handleClient(client) }
                } catch (_: Throwable) {
                    break
                }
            }
        }
        return "http://$ip:$port"
    }

    fun stop() {
        acceptJob?.cancel()
        acceptJob = null
        try {
            serverSocket?.close()
        } catch (_: Throwable) {}
        serverSocket = null
    }

    private fun handleClient(socket: Socket) {
        socket.use { sock ->
            sock.soTimeout = 10_000
            val input = sock.getInputStream()
            val output = sock.getOutputStream()

            val headerBytes = readUntilHeaderEnd(input) ?: return
            val headerText = String(headerBytes, Charsets.ISO_8859_1)
            val headerLines = headerText.split("\r\n")
            if (headerLines.isEmpty()) return

            val reqParts = headerLines[0].split(" ")
            if (reqParts.size < 2) return
            val method = reqParts[0].uppercase(Locale.US)
            val rawTarget = reqParts[1]
            val path = rawTarget.substringBefore('?')
            val queryStr = rawTarget.substringAfter('?', "")

            val headers = HashMap<String, String>()
            for (i in 1 until headerLines.size) {
                val line = headerLines[i]
                val colon = line.indexOf(':')
                if (colon > 0) {
                    headers[line.substring(0, colon).trim().lowercase(Locale.US)] =
                        line.substring(colon + 1).trim()
                }
            }

            val contentLength = headers["content-length"]?.toIntOrNull()?.coerceIn(0, 65536) ?: 0
            val bodyBytes = if (contentLength > 0) readExactBytes(input, contentLength) else ByteArray(0)
            val bodyText = String(bodyBytes, Charsets.UTF_8)

            when {
                method == "GET" && path == "/download" -> {
                    val params = parseFormParams(queryStr)
                    val fileName = params["name"]?.trim().orEmpty()
                    val file = CrashReporter.getReportFile(fileName)
                    if (file != null) {
                        val fileBytes = file.readBytes()
                        val safeName = file.name.replace("\"", "")
                        sendResponse(
                            output = output,
                            status = "200 OK",
                            contentType = "text/plain; charset=utf-8",
                            extraHeaders = listOf("Content-Disposition" to "attachment; filename=\"$safeName\""),
                            body = fileBytes
                        )
                    } else {
                        sendHtml(output, "404 Not Found", "<h3>Файл не найден</h3>")
                    }
                }

                method == "POST" && path == "/save" -> {
                    val form = parseFormParams(bodyText)
                    val token = form["token"]?.trim().orEmpty()
                    var chatId = form["chat_id"]?.trim().orEmpty()
                    val action = form["action"]?.trim().orEmpty()

                    var statusBanner: Pair<Boolean, String>
                    if (token.isBlank()) {
                        statusBanner = false to "Введите Bot Token от @BotFather."
                    } else {
                        if (chatId.isBlank() || action == "detect") {
                            val detectRes = runBlocking(Dispatchers.IO) {
                                CrashReporter.detectChatIdFromBot(token)
                            }
                            if (detectRes.isSuccess) {
                                val (foundId, foundName) = detectRes.getOrThrow()
                                chatId = foundId
                                CrashReporter.saveTelegramConfig(token, chatId)
                                val testRes = runBlocking(Dispatchers.IO) { CrashReporter.sendTestMessage() }
                                statusBanner = if (testRes.isSuccess) {
                                    true to "Chat ID найден ($foundName: $chatId) и тестовое сообщение отправлено!"
                                } else {
                                    false to "Chat ID найден ($chatId), но отправка не удалась: ${testRes.exceptionOrNull()?.message}"
                                }
                                onConfigUpdated()
                            } else {
                                CrashReporter.saveTelegramConfig(token, chatId)
                                onConfigUpdated()
                                statusBanner = false to (detectRes.exceptionOrNull()?.message ?: "Не удалось определить Chat ID")
                            }
                        } else {
                            CrashReporter.saveTelegramConfig(token, chatId)
                            onConfigUpdated()
                            val testRes = runBlocking(Dispatchers.IO) { CrashReporter.sendTestMessage() }
                            statusBanner = if (testRes.isSuccess) {
                                true to "Сохранено! Тестовое сообщение отправлено в Telegram."
                            } else {
                                false to "Сохранено, но ошибка отправки: ${testRes.exceptionOrNull()?.message}"
                            }
                        }
                    }

                    sendHtml(output, "200 OK", buildWebPageHtml(statusBanner))
                }

                method == "POST" && path == "/reset" -> {
                    CrashReporter.clearCustomConfig()
                    onConfigUpdated()
                    sendHtml(output, "200 OK", buildWebPageHtml(true to "Сброшено на встроенный канал отправки логов."))
                }

                method == "POST" && path == "/diag" -> {
                    val res = runBlocking(Dispatchers.IO) { CrashReporter.sendDiagnosticSnapshotNow() }
                    val banner = if (res.isSuccess) {
                        true to (res.getOrNull() ?: "Отправлено в Telegram!")
                    } else {
                        false to (res.exceptionOrNull()?.message ?: "Ошибка отправки")
                    }
                    onConfigUpdated()
                    sendHtml(output, "200 OK", buildWebPageHtml(banner))
                }

                else -> {
                    sendHtml(output, "200 OK", buildWebPageHtml(null))
                }
            }
        }
    }

    private fun readUntilHeaderEnd(input: InputStream): ByteArray? {
        val baos = ByteArrayOutputStream()
        var state = 0
        var count = 0
        while (count < 16384) {
            val b = input.read()
            if (b == -1) return if (baos.size() > 0) baos.toByteArray() else null
            baos.write(b)
            count++
            state = when (state) {
                0 -> if (b == '\r'.code) 1 else 0
                1 -> if (b == '\n'.code) 2 else if (b == '\r'.code) 1 else 0
                2 -> if (b == '\r'.code) 3 else 0
                3 -> if (b == '\n'.code) 4 else if (b == '\r'.code) 1 else 0
                else -> 0
            }
            if (state == 4) return baos.toByteArray()
        }
        return baos.toByteArray()
    }

    private fun readExactBytes(input: InputStream, length: Int): ByteArray {
        val buf = ByteArray(length)
        var offset = 0
        while (offset < length) {
            val read = input.read(buf, offset, length - offset)
            if (read <= 0) break
            offset += read
        }
        return if (offset == length) buf else buf.copyOf(offset)
    }

    private fun parseFormParams(raw: String): Map<String, String> {
        if (raw.isBlank()) return emptyMap()
        val map = LinkedHashMap<String, String>()
        for (pair in raw.split('&')) {
            if (pair.isBlank()) continue
            val eq = pair.indexOf('=')
            val key = if (eq >= 0) pair.substring(0, eq) else pair
            val valRaw = if (eq >= 0) pair.substring(eq + 1) else ""
            try {
                map[URLDecoder.decode(key, "UTF-8")] = URLDecoder.decode(valRaw, "UTF-8")
            } catch (_: Throwable) {}
        }
        return map
    }

    private fun escapeHtml(raw: String): String {
        return raw
            .replace("&", "&amp;")
            .replace("<", "&lt;")
            .replace(">", "&gt;")
            .replace("\"", "&quot;")
            .replace("'", "&#39;")
    }

    private fun buildWebPageHtml(statusBanner: Pair<Boolean, String>?): String {
        val currentToken = escapeHtml(CrashReporter.getCustomBotToken())
        val currentChatId = escapeHtml(CrashReporter.getCustomChatId())
        val hasBuiltIn = CrashReporter.hasBuiltInConfig()
        val hasCustom = CrashReporter.hasCustomOverride()
        val reports = CrashReporter.reports.value
        val df = SimpleDateFormat("dd.MM HH:mm:ss", Locale.getDefault())

        val builtInInfoHtml = if (hasBuiltIn && !hasCustom) {
            "<div style='padding:10px 12px;border-radius:10px;background:#102938;border:1px solid #00E5FF66;margin-bottom:14px;font-size:13px;color:#BAE6FD;'>" +
                "В эту сборку уже встроен канал отправки логов разработчику (токен скрыт в целях безопасности). Заполняйте форму ниже только если хотите перенаправить логи в своего личного бота." +
                "</div>"
        } else ""

        val resetBtnHtml = if (hasBuiltIn && hasCustom) {
            "<form method='POST' action='/reset' style='margin-top:10px;'>" +
                "<button class='secondary' type='submit' style='width:100%;'>Сбросить на встроенный канал разработчика</button>" +
                "</form>"
        } else ""

        val bannerHtml = if (statusBanner != null) {
            val bg = if (statusBanner.first) "#123828" else "#3B1920"
            val border = if (statusBanner.first) "#2EE59D" else "#FF5A79"
            "<div style='padding:12px 14px;border-radius:10px;background:$bg;border:1px solid $border;margin-bottom:16px;font-size:14px;'>${escapeHtml(statusBanner.second)}</div>"
        } else ""

        val reportsHtml = if (reports.isEmpty()) {
            "<p style='color:#94A3B8;font-size:14px;'>Сохранённых отчётов о сбоях пока нет.</p>"
        } else {
            reports.joinToString("") { r ->
                val dateLabel = df.format(Date(r.timestampMs))
                val badge = if (r.isSent) {
                    "<span style='color:#2EE59D;font-size:12px;'>Отправлен в ТГ</span>"
                } else {
                    "<span style='color:#FFB74D;font-size:12px;'>Ожидает отправки</span>"
                }
                val encodedName = URLEncoder.encode(r.fileName, "UTF-8")
                "<div style='padding:10px 12px;border-radius:10px;background:#1A202C;margin-bottom:8px;display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;gap:12px;'>" +
                    "<div style='min-width:0;flex:1;'>" +
                    "<div style='font-weight:600;font-size:14px;'>${escapeHtml(r.title)} · $dateLabel · $badge</div>" +
                    "<div style='color:#94A3B8;font-size:12px;white-space:nowrap;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;'>${escapeHtml(r.preview)}</div>" +
                    "</div>" +
                    "<a href='/download?name=$encodedName' style='color:#00E5FF;text-decoration:none;font-weight:600;font-size:13px;white-space:nowrap;'>Скачать .txt</a>" +
                    "</div>"
            }
        }

        return """
            <!DOCTYPE html>
            <html lang="ru">
            <head>
              <meta charset="utf-8" />
              <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1" />
              <title>Re:Player — Telegram логи</title>
              <style>
                body{font-family:-apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,Segoe UI,Roboto,sans-serif;background:#0D1117;color:#F1F5F9;margin:0;padding:18px;}
                .card{max-width:520px;margin:0 auto;background:#151B26;border:1px solid #263142;border-radius:16px;padding:20px;}
                h1{font-size:20px;margin:0 0 8px 0;color:#00E5FF;}
                p.hint{font-size:13px;color:#94A3B8;line-height:1.45;margin:0 0 16px 0;}
                label{display:block;font-size:13px;color:#CBD5E1;margin-bottom:6px;font-weight:600;}
                input{width:100%;box-sizing:border-box;padding:11px 12px;border-radius:10px;border:1px solid #334155;background:#0F141D;color:#F8FAFC;font-size:15px;margin-bottom:14px;}
                .row{display:flex;gap:10px;flex-wrap:wrap;}
                button{flex:1;min-width:140px;padding:12px 14px;border-radius:10px;border:none;font-size:14px;font-weight:700;cursor:pointer;}
                .primary{background:#00E5FF;color:#091017;}
                .secondary{background:#232C3B;color:#F8FAFC;border:1px solid #384457;}
                .section{margin-top:22px;padding-top:16px;border-top:1px solid #263142;}
              </style>
            </head>
            <body>
              <div class="card">
                <h1>Re:Player · Логи в Telegram</h1>
                <p class="hint">
                  1. Создайте бота в <b>@BotFather</b> (команда <code>/newbot</code>) и вставьте токен ниже.<br/>
                  2. Откройте созданного бота в Telegram и нажмите <b>Start</b> (отправьте <code>/start</code>).<br/>
                  3. Оставьте Chat ID пустым и нажмите «Найти Chat ID и сохранить».
                </p>
                $builtInInfoHtml
                $bannerHtml
                <form method="POST" action="/save">
                  <label>Bot Token</label>
                  <input type="text" name="token" value="$currentToken" placeholder="123456789:AAF..." required />
                  <label>Chat ID (необязательно — определится сам)</label>
                  <input type="text" name="chat_id" value="$currentChatId" placeholder="Автоопределение после /start" />
                  <div class="row">
                    <button class="primary" type="submit" name="action" value="detect">Найти Chat ID и сохранить</button>
                    <button class="secondary" type="submit" name="action" value="save">Сохранить и тест</button>
                  </div>
                </form>
                $resetBtnHtml

                <div class="section">
                  <form method="POST" action="/diag">
                    <button class="secondary" type="submit" style="width:100%;">Отправить текущий снимок логов сейчас</button>
                  </form>
                </div>

                <div class="section">
                  <label style="margin-bottom:10px;">Локальные отчёты о сбоях (${reports.size})</label>
                  $reportsHtml
                </div>
              </div>
            </body>
            </html>
        """.trimIndent()
    }

    private fun sendHtml(output: java.io.OutputStream, status: String, html: String) {
        sendResponse(output, status, "text/html; charset=utf-8", emptyList(), html.toByteArray(Charsets.UTF_8))
    }

    private fun sendResponse(
        output: java.io.OutputStream,
        status: String,
        contentType: String,
        extraHeaders: List<Pair<String, String>>,
        body: ByteArray
    ) {
        val sb = StringBuilder()
        sb.append("HTTP/1.1 $status\r\n")
        sb.append("Content-Type: $contentType\r\n")
        sb.append("Content-Length: ${body.size}\r\n")
        sb.append("Cache-Control: no-store\r\n")
        sb.append("Connection: close\r\n")
        for ((k, v) in extraHeaders) {
            sb.append("$k: $v\r\n")
        }
        sb.append("\r\n")
        output.write(sb.toString().toByteArray(Charsets.ISO_8859_1))
        output.write(body)
        output.flush()
    }

    private fun findLocalIpv4Address(): String? {
        return try {
            val candidates = mutableListOf<Pair<Int, String>>()
            val interfaces = NetworkInterface.getNetworkInterfaces() ?: return null
            while (interfaces.hasMoreElements()) {
                val nif = interfaces.nextElement()
                if (!nif.isUp || nif.isLoopback || nif.isVirtual) continue
                val name = nif.name.lowercase(Locale.US)
                val priority = when {
                    name.startsWith("eth") || name.startsWith("wlan") -> 0
                    name.startsWith("en") -> 1
                    name.contains("tun") || name.contains("p2p") || name.contains("rmnet") || name.contains("dummy") -> 9
                    else -> 3
                }
                val addrs = nif.inetAddresses
                while (addrs.hasMoreElements()) {
                    val addr = addrs.nextElement()
                    if (addr is Inet4Address && !addr.isLoopbackAddress && addr.isSiteLocalAddress) {
                        val host = addr.hostAddress ?: continue
                        candidates.add(priority to host)
                    }
                }
            }
            candidates.minByOrNull { it.first }?.second
        } catch (_: Throwable) {
            null
        }
    }
}

[ФАЙЛ 23: app/src/main/java/com/replayer/model/EpgProgram.kt] (49 lines)
================================================================================
package com.replayer.model

import kotlinx.serialization.Serializable

@Serializable
data class EpgProgram(
    val channelId: String,
    val title: String,
    val description: String? = null,
    val startTime: Long,
    val endTime: Long
) {
    fun isCurrent(now: Long = System.currentTimeMillis()): Boolean {
        return now in startTime..endTime
    }

    fun isPast(now: Long = System.currentTimeMillis()): Boolean {
        return endTime < now
    }

    fun isFuture(now: Long = System.currentTimeMillis()): Boolean {
        return startTime > now
    }

    fun durationMinutes(): Int {
        if (endTime <= startTime) return 0
        return ((endTime - startTime) / (60 * 1000L)).toInt()
    }

    fun progress(now: Long = System.currentTimeMillis()): Float {
        if (endTime <= startTime) return 0f
        return ((now - startTime).toFloat() / (endTime - startTime).toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
    }

    fun formattedTimeRange(): String {
        val s = java.text.SimpleDateFormat("HH:mm", java.util.Locale.getDefault())
        return "${s.format(java.util.Date(startTime))} - ${s.format(java.util.Date(endTime))}"
    }

    fun formattedStartTime(): String {
        val s = java.text.SimpleDateFormat("HH:mm", java.util.Locale.getDefault())
        return s.format(java.util.Date(startTime))
    }

    fun formattedEndTime(): String {
        val s = java.text.SimpleDateFormat("HH:mm", java.util.Locale.getDefault())
        return s.format(java.util.Date(endTime))
    }
}

package com.replayer.model

import kotlinx.serialization.Serializable

@Serializable
data class IptvChannel(
    val id: String,
    val name: String,
    val streamUrl: String,
    val logoUrl: String? = null,
    val group: String = "Все",
    val tvgId: String? = null,
    val tvgName: String? = null,
    val catchupType: String? = null,
    val catchupDays: Int = 0,
    val catchupSource: String? = null
) {
    val hasArchive: Boolean
        get() = catchupDays > 0 ||
            (!catchupType.isNullOrBlank() && !catchupType.equals("none", ignoreCase = true) && catchupDays >= 0) ||
            !catchupSource.isNullOrBlank()

    fun toMediaItem(): MediaItem = MediaItem(
        id = id,
        title = name,
        uri = streamUrl,
        type = MediaType.VIDEO,
        sourceType = MediaSourceType.IPTV,
        thumbnailUrl = logoUrl
    )
}

[ФАЙЛ 26: app/src/main/java/com/replayer/model/MediaItem.kt] (51 lines)
================================================================================
package com.replayer.model

import kotlinx.serialization.Serializable
import java.util.Locale

@Serializable
data class MediaItem(
    val id: String,
    val title: String,
    val uri: String,
    val type: MediaType,
    val sourceType: MediaSourceType,
    val size: Long = 0L,
    val durationMs: Long = 0L,
    val mimeType: String? = null,
    val thumbnailUrl: String? = null,
    val parentId: String? = null,
    val dateAdded: String? = null,
    val format: String? = null,
    val resolution: String? = null
) {
    fun formattedSize(): String {
        if (type == MediaType.DIRECTORY || size <= 0L) return ""
        val kb = size / 1024.0
        val mb = kb / 1024.0
        val gb = mb / 1024.0
        return when {
            gb >= 1.0 -> String.format(Locale.US, "%.2f ГБ", gb)
            mb >= 1.0 -> String.format(Locale.US, "%.1f МБ", mb)
            kb >= 1.0 -> String.format(Locale.US, "%.0f КБ", kb)
            else -> "$size Б"
        }
    }

    fun fileFormat(): String {
        if (type == MediaType.DIRECTORY) return "ПАПКА"
        if (!format.isNullOrEmpty()) return format.uppercase()
        val ext = title.substringAfterLast('.', "")
        return if (ext.isNotEmpty() && ext.length <= 5) ext.uppercase() else type.name
    }

    fun formattedDate(): String {
        if (dateAdded.isNullOrEmpty()) return ""
        // If it's ISO like 2023-05-12T14:30:00 -> take 2023-05-12
        return if (dateAdded.contains('T')) {
            dateAdded.substringBefore('T')
        } else {
            dateAdded.take(10)
        }
    }
}

@Serializable
data class RecentMedia(

    )

[ФАЙЛ 33: app/src/main/java/com/replayer/network/iptv/M3uParser.kt] (170 lines)
================================================================================
package com.replayer.network.iptv

import com.replayer.model.IptvChannel
import java.io.BufferedReader
import java.io.InputStream
import java.io.InputStreamReader

data class M3uParseResult(
    val channels: List<IptvChannel>,
    val epgUrl: String?
)

object M3uParser {

    private fun extractAttr(line: String, vararg attrNames: String): String? {
        val pattern = attrNames.joinToString("|") { Regex.escape(it) }
        val regex = Regex(
            """(?:^|[\s,])(?:$pattern)\s*=\s*(?:"([^"]*)"|'([^']*)'|([^\s,]*))""",
            RegexOption.IGNORE_CASE
        )
        val match = regex.find(line) ?: return null
        val value = match.groupValues.getOrNull(1)?.takeIf { it.isNotEmpty() }
            ?: match.groupValues.getOrNull(2)?.takeIf { it.isNotEmpty() }
            ?: match.groupValues.getOrNull(3)?.takeIf { it.isNotEmpty() }
        return value?.trim()?.ifBlank { null }
    }

    /**
     * Finds the channel title after the first comma outside quotes in #EXTINF line.
     */
    private fun extractTitle(extInf: String): String? {
        var inSingleQuote = false
        var inDoubleQuote = false
        val colonIdx = extInf.indexOf(':')
        val startIdx = if (colonIdx != -1) colonIdx + 1 else 0

        for (i in startIdx until extInf.length) {
            val c = extInf[i]
            when {
                c == '"' && !inSingleQuote -> inDoubleQuote = !inDoubleQuote
                c == '\'' && !inDoubleQuote -> inSingleQuote = !inSingleQuote
                c == ',' && !inSingleQuote && !inDoubleQuote -> {
                    val rawTitle = extInf.substring(i + 1).trim()
                    return rawTitle.ifBlank { null }
                }
            }
        }
        return null
    }

    fun parse(inputStream: InputStream): M3uParseResult {
        val reader = BufferedReader(InputStreamReader(inputStream, Charsets.UTF_8))
        val channels = mutableListOf<IptvChannel>()
        val usedIds = mutableSetOf<String>()
        var detectedEpgUrl: String? = null

        var globalCatchupType: String? = null
        var globalCatchupDays: Int? = null
        var globalCatchupSource: String? = null

        var currentExtInf: String? = null
        var perItemGroup: String? = null
        var perItemLogo: String? = null
        var sectionGroup: String? = null
        var index = 0

        reader.forEachLine { rawLine ->
            // Strip UTF-8 BOM if present
            val line = rawLine.trim().removePrefix("\uFEFF")
            if (line.isEmpty()) return@forEachLine

            when {
                line.startsWith("#EXTM3U", ignoreCase = true) -> {
                    extractAttr(line, "url-tvg", "x-tvg-url")?.let { raw ->
                        val firstUrl = raw.split(",", ";").firstOrNull()?.trim()
                        if (!firstUrl.isNullOrBlank()) {
                            detectedEpgUrl = firstUrl
                        }
                    }
                    extractAttr(line, "catchup", "catchup-type")?.let { globalCatchupType = it.lowercase() }
                    extractAttr(line, "tvg-rec", "catchup-days", "timeshift")?.toIntOrNull()?.let {
                        globalCatchupDays = it.coerceAtLeast(0)
                    }
                    extractAttr(line, "catchup-source")?.let { globalCatchupSource = it }
                }
                line.startsWith("#EXTINF:", ignoreCase = true) || line.equals("#EXTINF", ignoreCase = true) -> {
                    currentExtInf = line
                    perItemGroup = null
                    perItemLogo = null
                }
                line.startsWith("#EXTGRP:", ignoreCase = true) -> {
                    val grp = line.substringAfter(":", "").trim().ifBlank { null }
                    if (currentExtInf != null) {
                        perItemGroup = grp
                    } else {
                        sectionGroup = grp
                    }
                }
                line.startsWith("#EXTIMG:", ignoreCase = true) ||
                    line.startsWith("#EXTALBUMARTURL:", ignoreCase = true) -> {
                    val img = line.substringAfter(":", "").trim().ifBlank { null }
                    if (currentExtInf != null) {
                        perItemLogo = img
                    }
                }
                !line.startsWith("#") -> {
                    val extInf = currentExtInf
                    val streamUrl = line

                    val tvgId = extInf?.let { extractAttr(it, "tvg-id", "channel-id") }
                    val tvgName = extInf?.let { extractAttr(it, "tvg-name") }
                    val extInfLogo = extInf?.let { extractAttr(it, "tvg-logo", "tvg-icon", "logo") }
                    val extInfGroup = extInf?.let { extractAttr(it, "group-title", "tvg-group", "group") }

                    val itemCatchupType = extInf?.let { extractAttr(it, "catchup", "catchup-type") }?.lowercase()
                    val itemDays = extInf?.let { extractAttr(it, "tvg-rec", "catchup-days", "timeshift") }?.toIntOrNull()
                    val itemCatchupSource = extInf?.let { extractAttr(it, "catchup-source") }

                    val (finalCatchupType, finalCatchupDays, finalCatchupSource) = when {
                        itemDays == 0 || itemCatchupType == "none" -> Triple(null, 0, null)
                        else -> {
                            val type = itemCatchupType ?: globalCatchupType
                            val src = itemCatchupSource ?: globalCatchupSource
                            val days = (itemDays ?: globalCatchupDays ?: if (type != null || src != null) 3 else 0)
                                .coerceIn(0, 14)
                            Triple(type, days, src)
                        }
                    }

                    val title = extInf?.let { extractTitle(it) } ?: tvgName ?: "Канал ${index + 1}"
                    val groupTitle = (extInfGroup ?: perItemGroup ?: sectionGroup)?.trim()?.ifBlank { null } ?: "Общие"
                    val finalLogo = (extInfLogo ?: perItemLogo)?.trim()?.ifBlank { null }

                    if (title.isNotBlank() && streamUrl.isNotBlank()) {
                        index++
                        val baseId = tvgId ?: "ch_${index}_${title.hashCode()}"
                        val uniqueId = if (usedIds.add(baseId)) {
                            baseId
                        } else {
                            val disambiguated = "${baseId}_$index"
                            usedIds.add(disambiguated)
                            disambiguated
                        }

                        channels.add(
                            IptvChannel(
                                id = uniqueId,
                                name = title,
                                streamUrl = streamUrl,
                                logoUrl = finalLogo,
                                group = groupTitle,
                                tvgId = tvgId,
                                tvgName = tvgName,
                                catchupType = finalCatchupType,
                                catchupDays = finalCatchupDays,
                                catchupSource = finalCatchupSource
                            )
                        )
                    }

                    currentExtInf = null
                    perItemGroup = null
                    perItemLogo = null
                }
            }
        }

        return M3uParseResult(channels = channels, epgUrl = detectedEpgUrl)
    }
}

[ФАЙЛ 34: app/src/main/java/com/replayer/network/iptv/XmlTvParser.kt] (312 lines)
================================================================================
package com.replayer.network.iptv

import android.util.Xml
import com.replayer.model.EpgProgram
import org.xmlpull.v1.XmlPullParser
import java.io.BufferedInputStream
import java.io.InputStream
import java.text.SimpleDateFormat
import java.util.Locale
import java.util.zip.GZIPInputStream

data class XmlTvParseResult(
    val programs: Map<String, List<EpgProgram>>,
    val logos: Map<String, String>
)

object XmlTvParser {

    fun parse(
        rawInputStream: InputStream,
        targetChannels: Set<String>? = null,
        archiveChannels: Set<String>? = null,
        onProgress: ((String) -> Unit)? = null
    ): XmlTvParseResult {
        // Detect GZIP by magic bytes (0x1f, 0x8b)
        val buffered = BufferedInputStream(rawInputStream, 65536)
        buffered.mark(4)
        val b1 = buffered.read()
        val b2 = buffered.read()
        buffered.reset()

        val isGzip = (b1 == 0x1f && b2 == 0x8b)
        val stream: InputStream = if (isGzip) GZIPInputStream(buffered) else buffered

        val parser = Xml.newPullParser()
        parser.setInput(stream, "UTF-8")

        // Mapping from display name / alias (lowercased) -> channel XML ID
        val aliasToChannelId = mutableMapOf<String, String>()
        // All XML IDs that matched the user's target channels (or all channels if targetChannels is null)
        val relevantXmlChannelIds = mutableSetOf<String>()
        // XML IDs that support catchup archive (load -3 days only for these channels!)
        val archiveXmlChannelIds = mutableSetOf<String>()
        // Channel logos from <icon src="..."> inside <channel>
        val channelIcons = mutableMapOf<String, String>()
        // Canonical channel programs: channelId -> list of programs
        val channelPrograms = mutableMapOf<String, MutableList<EpgProgram>>()

        val now = System.currentTimeMillis()
        val archiveMinTime = now - 3L * 24 * 3600 * 1000L // last 3 days ONLY for archive channels
        val liveMinTime = now - 2L * 3600 * 1000L // last 2h for non-archive channels
        val maxTime = now + 36L * 3600 * 1000L // next 36h

        var currentChannelId: String? = null
        var totalProgrammesCount = 0
        var eventType = parser.eventType

        while (eventType != XmlPullParser.END_DOCUMENT) {
            when (eventType) {
                XmlPullParser.START_TAG -> {
                    when (parser.name) {
                        "channel" -> {
                            currentChannelId = parser.getAttributeValue(null, "id")?.trim()
                            if (!currentChannelId.isNullOrBlank()) {
                                val idLower = currentChannelId.lowercase()
                                val idClean = normalizeChannelName(currentChannelId)
                                if (targetChannels == null) {
                                    relevantXmlChannelIds.add(currentChannelId)
                                } else if (targetChannels.contains(currentChannelId) ||
                                    targetChannels.contains(idLower) ||
                                    (idClean.isNotEmpty() && targetChannels.contains(idClean))
                                ) {
                                    relevantXmlChannelIds.add(currentChannelId)
                                }

                                if (archiveChannels == null ||
                                    archiveChannels.contains(currentChannelId) ||
                                    archiveChannels.contains(idLower) ||
                                    (idClean.isNotEmpty() && archiveChannels.contains(idClean))
                                ) {
                                    archiveXmlChannelIds.add(currentChannelId)
                                }
                            }
                        }
                        "display-name" -> {
                            if (!currentChannelId.isNullOrBlank()) {
                                val name = parser.nextText().trim()
                                if (name.isNotEmpty()) {
                                    val lower = name.lowercase()
                                    aliasToChannelId[lower] = currentChannelId
                                    val clean = normalizeChannelName(name)
                                    if (clean.isNotEmpty()) {
                                        aliasToChannelId[clean] = currentChannelId
                                    }

                                    // Check if this display-name matches any target channel
                                    if (targetChannels != null) {
                                        if (targetChannels.contains(lower) ||
                                            targetChannels.contains(name) ||
                                            (clean.isNotEmpty() && targetChannels.contains(clean))
                                        ) {
                                            relevantXmlChannelIds.add(currentChannelId)
                                        }
                                    }
                                    // Check if this display-name matches an archive-capable channel
                                    if (archiveChannels != null) {
                                        if (archiveChannels.contains(lower) ||
                                            archiveChannels.contains(name) ||
                                            (clean.isNotEmpty() && archiveChannels.contains(clean))
                                        ) {
                                            archiveXmlChannelIds.add(currentChannelId)
                                        }
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        }
                        "icon" -> {
                            if (!currentChannelId.isNullOrBlank()) {
                                val src = parser.getAttributeValue(null, "src")?.trim()
                                if (!src.isNullOrBlank() && !channelIcons.containsKey(currentChannelId)) {
                                    channelIcons[currentChannelId] = src
                                }
                            }
                        }
                        "programme" -> {
                            val channel = parser.getAttributeValue(null, "channel")?.trim()
                            val startStr = parser.getAttributeValue(null, "start")?.trim()
                            val stopStr = parser.getAttributeValue(null, "stop")?.trim()

                            // Check if this programme belongs to a matched relevant channel
                            val isRelevant = if (targetChannels != null) {
                                !channel.isNullOrBlank() && relevantXmlChannelIds.contains(channel)
                            } else {
                                !channel.isNullOrBlank()
                            }

                            if (!isRelevant || channel.isNullOrBlank() || startStr.isNullOrBlank() || stopStr.isNullOrBlank()) {
                                skipTag(parser)
                            } else {
                                val start = parseXmlDate(startStr)
                                val stop = parseXmlDate(stopStr)
                                val effectiveMinTime = if (archiveChannels == null || archiveXmlChannelIds.contains(channel)) {
                                    archiveMinTime
                                } else {
                                    liveMinTime
                                }

                                if (start != null && stop != null && stop >= effectiveMinTime && start <= maxTime) {
                                    var title: String? = null
                                    var desc: String? = null

                                    while (true) {
                                        val innerEvent = parser.next()
                                        if (innerEvent == XmlPullParser.START_TAG) {
                                            when (parser.name) {
                                                "title" -> title = parser.nextText().trim()
                                                "desc" -> desc = parser.nextText().trim()
                                                else -> skipTag(parser)
                                            }
                                        } else if (innerEvent == XmlPullParser.END_TAG && parser.name == "programme") {
                                            break
                                        } else if (innerEvent == XmlPullParser.END_DOCUMENT) {
                                            break
                                        }
                                    }

                                    if (!title.isNullOrBlank()) {
                                        val list = channelPrograms.getOrPut(channel) { mutableListOf() }
                                        list.add(
                                            EpgProgram(
                                                channelId = channel,
                                                title = title,
                                                description = desc?.take(1200),
                                                startTime = start,
                                                endTime = stop
                                            )
                                        )
                                        totalProgrammesCount++
                                        if (totalProgrammesCount % 500 == 0) {
                                            onProgress?.invoke("Индексация: $totalProgrammesCount передач...")
                                        }
                                    }
                                } else {
                                    skipTag(parser)
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
                XmlPullParser.END_TAG -> {
                    if (parser.name == "channel") {
                        currentChannelId = null
                    }
                }
            }
            eventType = parser.next()
        }

        // Sort canonical lists by startTime
        val sortedCanonical = channelPrograms.mapValues { (_, progs) ->
            progs.sortedBy { it.startTime }
        }

        // Build composite lookup map:
        // 1. All original channel IDs (case-sensitive and lowercase)
        // 2. All display-name aliases pointing to their programs
        val finalMap = mutableMapOf<String, List<EpgProgram>>()

        for ((chId, progs) in sortedCanonical) {
            finalMap[chId] = progs
            finalMap[chId.lowercase()] = progs
            val cleanId = normalizeChannelName(chId)
            if (cleanId.isNotEmpty()) {
                finalMap[cleanId] = progs
            }
        }

        for ((alias, chId) in aliasToChannelId) {
            val progs = sortedCanonical[chId]
            if (progs != null && progs.isNotEmpty()) {
                finalMap.putIfAbsent(alias, progs)
                val clean = normalizeChannelName(alias)
                if (clean.isNotEmpty()) {
                    finalMap.putIfAbsent(clean, progs)
                }
            }
        }

        val finalLogos = mutableMapOf<String, String>()
        for ((chId, logo) in channelIcons) {
            if (targetChannels == null || relevantXmlChannelIds.contains(chId)) {
                finalLogos[chId] = logo
                finalLogos[chId.lowercase()] = logo
                val cleanId = normalizeChannelName(chId)
                if (cleanId.isNotEmpty()) {
                    finalLogos[cleanId] = logo
                }
            }
        }
        for ((alias, chId) in aliasToChannelId) {
            if (targetChannels == null || relevantXmlChannelIds.contains(chId)) {
                val logo = channelIcons[chId]
                if (!logo.isNullOrBlank()) {
                    finalLogos.putIfAbsent(alias, logo)
                    val clean = normalizeChannelName(alias)
                    if (clean.isNotEmpty()) {
                        finalLogos.putIfAbsent(clean, logo)
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        return XmlTvParseResult(programs = finalMap, logos = finalLogos)
    }

    private fun skipTag(parser: XmlPullParser) {
        if (parser.eventType != XmlPullParser.START_TAG) return
        var depth = 1
        while (depth != 0) {
            when (parser.next()) {
                XmlPullParser.END_TAG -> depth--
                XmlPullParser.START_TAG -> depth++
            }
        }
    }

    fun normalizeChannelName(raw: String): String {
        return raw.lowercase()
            .replace(Regex("\\[.*?\\]|\\(.*?\\)"), "")
            .replace(Regex("\\b(hd|fhd|4k|uhd|sd|50fps|orig|hevc)\\b"), "")
            .replace(Regex("[^a-zA-Z0-9а-яА-ЯёЁ]"), "")
            .trim()
    }

    /**
     * Blazing fast, thread-safe XMLTV date parser without SimpleDateFormat allocations:
     * - "20261103120000 +0300"
     * - "20261103120000 +03:00"
     * - "20261103120000"
     */
    fun parseXmlDate(dateStr: String): Long? {
        val s = dateStr.trim()
        if (s.length < 14) return null
        return try {
            val y = s.substring(0, 4).toInt()
            val m = s.substring(4, 6).toInt()
            val d = s.substring(6, 8).toInt()
            val h = s.substring(8, 10).toInt()
            val min = s.substring(10, 12).toInt()
            val sec = s.substring(12, 14).toInt()

            var offsetMinutes = 0
            val rest = s.substring(14).trim()
            if (rest.isNotEmpty() && (rest.startsWith("+") || rest.startsWith("-"))) {
                val sign = if (rest[0] == '+') 1 else -1
                val cleanTz = rest.substring(1).replace(":", "")
                if (cleanTz.length >= 4) {
                    val tzH = cleanTz.substring(0, 2).toInt()
                    val tzM = cleanTz.substring(2, 4).toInt()
                    offsetMinutes = sign * (tzH * 60 + tzM)
                }
            }

            val cal = java.util.Calendar.getInstance(java.util.TimeZone.getTimeZone("UTC"))
            cal.set(y, m - 1, d, h, min, sec)
            cal.set(java.util.Calendar.MILLISECOND, 0)
            cal.timeInMillis - (offsetMinutes * 60 * 1000L)
        } catch (_: Exception) {
            null
        }
    }
}

[ФАЙЛ 35: app/src/main/java/com/replayer/network/upnp/ContentDirectoryService.kt] (334 lines)
================================================================================
package com.replayer.network.upnp

import android.util.Log
import android.util.Xml
import com.replayer.model.MediaItem
import com.replayer.model.MediaSourceType
import com.replayer.model.MediaType
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext
import okhttp3.MediaType.Companion.toMediaType
import okhttp3.OkHttpClient
import okhttp3.Request
import okhttp3.RequestBody.Companion.toRequestBody
import org.xmlpull.v1.XmlPullParser
import java.io.StringReader

class ContentDirectoryService {
    private val client = OkHttpClient()

    private data class SoapDidlResponse(
        val didlXml: String,
        val numberReturned: Int,
        val totalMatches: Int
    )

    companion object {
        private val UNESCAPED_AMP_REGEX = Regex("&(?!amp;|lt;|gt;|quot;|apos;|#\\d+;|#x[0-9a-fA-F]+;)")
        private val RESULT_FALLBACK_REGEX = Regex("<(?:[^:>\\s]+:)?Result[^>]*>(.*?)</(?:[^:>\\s]+:)?Result>", setOf(RegexOption.DOT_MATCHES_ALL, RegexOption.IGNORE_CASE))
        private val NUM_RETURNED_REGEX = Regex("<(?:[^:>\\s]+:)?NumberReturned[^>]*>(\\d+)</", RegexOption.IGNORE_CASE)
        private val TOTAL_MATCHES_REGEX = Regex("<(?:[^:>\\s]+:)?TotalMatches[^>]*>(\\d+)</", RegexOption.IGNORE_CASE)
    }

    suspend fun browse(controlUrl: String, objectId: String = "0"): List<MediaItem> = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        val items = mutableListOf<MediaItem>()
        val pageSize = 200
        var startingIndex = 0
        var totalMatches = Int.MAX_VALUE
        val safeMaxItems = 2000

        try {
            while (items.size < totalMatches && items.size < safeMaxItems) {
                val soapAction = "\"urn:schemas-upnp-org:service:ContentDirectory:1#Browse\""
                val soapBody = buildString {
                    append("<?xml version=\"1.0\" encoding=\"utf-8\"?>")
                    append("<s:Envelope xmlns:s=\"http://schemas.xmlsoap.org/soap/envelope/\" s:encodingStyle=\"http://schemas.xmlsoap.org/soap/encoding/\">")
                    append("<s:Body>")
                    append("<u:Browse xmlns:u=\"urn:schemas-upnp-org:service:ContentDirectory:1\">")
                    append("<ObjectID>${escapeXmlText(objectId)}</ObjectID>")
                    append("<BrowseFlag>BrowseDirectChildren</BrowseFlag>")
                    append("<Filter>*</Filter>")
                    append("<StartingIndex>$startingIndex</StartingIndex>")
                    append("<RequestedCount>$pageSize</RequestedCount>")
                    append("<SortCriteria></SortCriteria>")
                    append("</u:Browse>")
                    append("</s:Body>")
                    append("</s:Envelope>")
                }

                val pageResp = executeSoapDidlRequest(controlUrl, soapAction, soapBody) ?: break
                val beforeCount = items.size
                parseDidlLite(pageResp.didlXml, items, objectId)
                val parsedThisPage = items.size - beforeCount

                if (pageResp.totalMatches > 0) {
                    totalMatches = pageResp.totalMatches
                }
                val advanced = maxOf(pageResp.numberReturned, parsedThisPage)
                if (advanced <= 0 || parsedThisPage == 0) break
                startingIndex += advanced
                if (pageResp.totalMatches <= 0 && parsedThisPage < pageSize) break
            }
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e("ContentDirectoryService", "Browse error for $controlUrl (obj=$objectId)", e)
        }

        items
    }

    suspend fun search(
        controlUrl: String,
        containerId: String = "0",
        searchCriteria: String,
        maxItems: Int = 200
    ): List<MediaItem> = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        val items = mutableListOf<MediaItem>()
        try {
            val soapAction = "\"urn:schemas-upnp-org:service:ContentDirectory:1#Search\""
            val soapBody = buildString {
                append("<?xml version=\"1.0\" encoding=\"utf-8\"?>")
                append("<s:Envelope xmlns:s=\"http://schemas.xmlsoap.org/soap/envelope/\" s:encodingStyle=\"http://schemas.xmlsoap.org/soap/encoding/\">")
                append("<s:Body>")
                append("<u:Search xmlns:u=\"urn:schemas-upnp-org:service:ContentDirectory:1\">")
                append("<ContainerID>${escapeXmlText(containerId)}</ContainerID>")
                append("<SearchCriteria>${escapeXmlText(searchCriteria)}</SearchCriteria>")
                append("<Filter>*</Filter>")
                append("<StartingIndex>0</StartingIndex>")
                append("<RequestedCount>$maxItems</RequestedCount>")
                append("<SortCriteria></SortCriteria>")
                append("</u:Search>")
                append("</s:Body>")
                append("</s:Envelope>")
            }

            val resp = executeSoapDidlRequest(controlUrl, soapAction, soapBody)
            if (resp != null) {
                parseDidlLite(resp.didlXml, items, containerId)
            }
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.w("ContentDirectoryService", "Search not supported or failed on $controlUrl: ${e.message}")
        }
        items
    }

    private fun executeSoapDidlRequest(
        controlUrl: String,
        soapAction: String,
        soapBody: String
    ): SoapDidlResponse? {
        val request = Request.Builder()
            .url(controlUrl)
            .addHeader("SOAPAction", soapAction)
            .post(soapBody.toRequestBody("text/xml; charset=utf-8".toMediaType()))
            .build()

        client.newCall(request).execute().use { response ->
            if (!response.isSuccessful) return null
            val rawXml = response.body?.string() ?: return null
            return extractSoapResponse(rawXml)
        }
    }

    private fun escapeXmlText(text: String): String {
        return text
            .replace("&", "&amp;")
            .replace("<", "&lt;")
            .replace(">", "&gt;")
            .replace("\"", "&quot;")
            .replace("'", "&apos;")
    }

    private fun extractSoapResponse(soapXml: String): SoapDidlResponse? {
        try {
            val parser = Xml.newPullParser()
            parser.setFeature(XmlPullParser.FEATURE_PROCESS_NAMESPACES, false)
            parser.setInput(StringReader(soapXml))
            var didl: String? = null
            var numReturned = 0
            var totalMatches = 0
            var event = parser.eventType
            while (event != XmlPullParser.END_DOCUMENT) {
                if (event == XmlPullParser.START_TAG) {
                    val localTag = (parser.name ?: "").substringAfterLast(':').lowercase()
                    when (localTag) {
                        "result" -> didl = parser.nextText()
                        "numberreturned" -> numReturned = parser.nextText().trim().toIntOrNull() ?: 0
                        "totalmatches" -> totalMatches = parser.nextText().trim().toIntOrNull() ?: 0
                    }
                }
                event = parser.next()
            }
            if (!didl.isNullOrBlank()) {
                return SoapDidlResponse(didl, numReturned, totalMatches)
            }
        } catch (_: Throwable) {
            // Fallback to regex extraction if outer SOAP envelope is malformed
        }

        val resultMatch = RESULT_FALLBACK_REGEX.find(soapXml) ?: return null
        val encoded = resultMatch.groupValues[1]
        val didl = encoded
            .replace("&lt;", "<")
            .replace("&gt;", ">")
            .replace("&quot;", "\"")
            .replace("&apos;", "'")
            .replace("&amp;", "&")
        val numRet = NUM_RETURNED_REGEX.find(soapXml)?.groupValues?.getOrNull(1)?.toIntOrNull() ?: 0
        val totMat = TOTAL_MATCHES_REGEX.find(soapXml)?.groupValues?.getOrNull(1)?.toIntOrNull() ?: 0
        return SoapDidlResponse(didl, numRet, totMat)
    }

    private fun parseDidlLite(didlXml: String, items: MutableList<MediaItem>, defaultParentId: String) {
        val sanitizedXml = didlXml.replace(UNESCAPED_AMP_REGEX, "&amp;")
        val parser = Xml.newPullParser()
        parser.setFeature(XmlPullParser.FEATURE_PROCESS_NAMESPACES, false)
        parser.setInput(StringReader(sanitizedXml))

        var eventType = parser.eventType
        var currentItem: MutableMap<String, String>? = null
        var currentResIsThumb = false
        var currentResProto = ""
        val textBuffer = StringBuilder()

        while (eventType != XmlPullParser.END_DOCUMENT) {
            when (eventType) {
                XmlPullParser.START_TAG -> {
                    val rawName = parser.name ?: ""
                    val localTag = rawName.substringAfterLast(':').lowercase()
                    textBuffer.clear()

                    if (localTag == "container" || localTag == "item") {
                        currentItem = mutableMapOf()
                        currentResIsThumb = false
                        currentResProto = ""
                        currentItem["isContainer"] = if (localTag == "container") "true" else "false"
                        currentItem["id"] = parser.getAttributeValue(null, "id") ?: ""
                        val attrParent = parser.getAttributeValue(null, "parentID")
                        currentItem["parentID"] = if (!attrParent.isNullOrBlank()) attrParent else defaultParentId
                    }
                    if (localTag == "res" && currentItem != null) {
                        val proto = parser.getAttributeValue(null, "protocolInfo") ?: ""
                        val sizeAttr = parser.getAttributeValue(null, "size")
                        val resAttr = parser.getAttributeValue(null, "resolution")
                        val upnpCls = currentItem["class"] ?: ""
                        currentResProto = proto
                        currentResIsThumb = proto.contains("JPEG_TN", ignoreCase = true) ||
                            proto.contains("PNG_TN", ignoreCase = true) ||
                            (upnpCls.contains("videoItem", ignoreCase = true) && proto.contains("image/", ignoreCase = true))

                        if (!currentResIsThumb) {
                            if (proto.isNotBlank() && (!currentItem.containsKey("protocolInfo") || !proto.contains("image/", ignoreCase = true))) {
                                currentItem["protocolInfo"] = proto
                            }
                            if (!sizeAttr.isNullOrEmpty() && !currentItem.containsKey("size")) {
                                currentItem["size"] = sizeAttr
                            }
                            if (!resAttr.isNullOrEmpty() && (!currentItem.containsKey("resolution") || !proto.contains("image/", ignoreCase = true))) {
                                currentItem["resolution"] = resAttr
                            }
                        } else if (!resAttr.isNullOrEmpty() && !currentItem.containsKey("albumArtResolution")) {
                            currentItem["albumArtResolution"] = resAttr
                        }
                    }
                }
                XmlPullParser.TEXT -> {
                    textBuffer.append(parser.text ?: "")
                }
                XmlPullParser.END_TAG -> {
                    val rawName = parser.name ?: ""
                    val localTag = rawName.substringAfterLast(':').lowercase()
                    val text = textBuffer.toString().trim()

                    if (currentItem != null && text.isNotEmpty()) {
                        when (localTag) {
                            "title" -> currentItem["title"] = text
                            "res" -> {
                                val existingProto = currentItem["resProto"] ?: ""
                                if (currentResIsThumb) {
                                    if (!currentItem.containsKey("albumArt")) {
                                        currentItem["albumArt"] = text
                                    }
                                } else if (!currentItem.containsKey("res")) {
                                    currentItem["res"] = text
                                    currentItem["resProto"] = currentResProto
                                } else if (existingProto.contains("image/", ignoreCase = true) &&
                                    (currentResProto.contains("video/", ignoreCase = true) || currentResProto.contains("audio/", ignoreCase = true))
                                ) {
                                    // Prefer primary video/audio stream over thumbnail if <res> appeared before <upnp:class>
                                    if (!currentItem.containsKey("albumArt")) {
                                        currentItem["albumArt"] = currentItem["res"] ?: ""
                                    }
                                    currentItem["res"] = text
                                    currentItem["resProto"] = currentResProto
                                }
                            }
                            "class" -> currentItem["class"] = text
                            "date" -> currentItem["date"] = text
                            "albumarturi" -> if (!currentItem.containsKey("albumArt")) currentItem["albumArt"] = text
                        }
                    }

                    if ((localTag == "container" || localTag == "item") && currentItem != null) {
                        val isContainer = currentItem["isContainer"] == "true"
                        val upnpClass = currentItem["class"] ?: ""
                        val title = currentItem["title"]?.trim()?.ifEmpty { "Unnamed" } ?: "Unnamed"
                        val id = currentItem["id"] ?: ""
                        val itemParentId = currentItem["parentID"] ?: defaultParentId
                        val rawRes = currentItem["res"] ?: ""
                        val size = currentItem["size"]?.toLongOrNull() ?: 0L
                        val date = currentItem["date"]
                        val albumArt = currentItem["albumArt"]
                        val resolution = currentItem["resolution"]
                        val proto = currentItem["protocolInfo"] ?: currentItem["resProto"] ?: ""
                        val res = rawRes.ifBlank { if (!isContainer) (albumArt ?: "") else "" }
                        val resClean = res.substringBefore('?').substringBefore('#')
                        val titleLower = title.lowercase()
                        val resLower = resClean.lowercase()
                        fun hasAnyExt(vararg exts: String): Boolean =
                            exts.any { titleLower.endsWith(it) || resLower.endsWith(it) }

                        val ext = title.substringAfterLast('.', "").uppercase()
                        val format = if (ext.isNotEmpty() && ext.length <= 5) ext else null

                        val mediaType = when {
                            isContainer -> MediaType.DIRECTORY
                            upnpClass.contains("imageItem", ignoreCase = true) ||
                                (!upnpClass.contains("videoItem", ignoreCase = true) &&
                                    !upnpClass.contains("audioItem", ignoreCase = true) &&
                                    (proto.contains("image/", ignoreCase = true) ||
                                        hasAnyExt(".jpg", ".jpeg", ".png", ".webp", ".bmp", ".gif"))) -> MediaType.IMAGE
                            hasAnyExt(".srt", ".ass", ".ssa", ".sub", ".vtt", ".idx") ||
                                proto.contains("text/", ignoreCase = true) -> MediaType.UNKNOWN
                            upnpClass.contains("audioItem", ignoreCase = true) ||
                                proto.contains("audio/", ignoreCase = true) ||
                                hasAnyExt(".mp3", ".flac", ".m4a", ".wav", ".aac", ".ogg", ".opus", ".wma") -> MediaType.AUDIO
                            upnpClass.contains("videoItem", ignoreCase = true) ||
                                proto.contains("video/", ignoreCase = true) ||
                                hasAnyExt(".mkv", ".mp4", ".avi", ".ts", ".m2ts", ".mov", ".webm", ".m4v", ".wmv", ".mpg", ".mpeg", ".flv") -> MediaType.VIDEO
                            else -> MediaType.UNKNOWN
                        }

                        items.add(
                            MediaItem(
                                id = id,
                                title = title,
                                uri = res,
                                type = mediaType,
                                sourceType = MediaSourceType.UPNP_DLNA,
                                size = size,
                                thumbnailUrl = albumArt,
                                parentId = itemParentId,
                                dateAdded = date,
                                format = format,
                                resolution = resolution
                            )
                        )
                        currentItem = null
                    }
                    textBuffer.clear()
                }
            }
            eventType = parser.next()
        }
    }
}

[ФАЙЛ 36: app/src/main/java/com/replayer/network/upnp/SsdpDiscovery.kt] (97 lines)

package com.replayer.network.upnp

import android.content.Context
import android.net.wifi.WifiManager
import android.util.Log
import com.replayer.model.UpnpDevice
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.Flow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.flow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.flowOn
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext
import java.net.DatagramPacket
import java.net.DatagramSocket
import java.net.InetAddress
import java.net.SocketTimeoutException

class SsdpDiscovery(private val context: Context) {

    companion object {
        private const val TAG = "SsdpDiscovery"
        private const val SSDP_IP = "239.255.255.250"
        private const val SSDP_PORT = 1900
        private const val SEARCH_TARGET = "urn:schemas-upnp-org:service:ContentDirectory:1"
    }

    fun discoverDevices(timeoutMs: Int = 4000): Flow<List<UpnpDevice>> = flow {
        val wifiManager = context.applicationContext.getSystemService(Context.WIFI_SERVICE) as WifiManager
        val multicastLock = wifiManager.createMulticastLock("RePlayerSsdpLock").apply {
            setReferenceCounted(true)
            acquire()
        }

        val discoveredMap = mutableMapOf<String, UpnpDevice>()

        try {
            val searchMessage = buildString {
                append("M-SEARCH * HTTP/1.1\r\n")
                append("HOST: $SSDP_IP:$SSDP_PORT\r\n")
                append("MAN: \"ssdp:discover\"\r\n")
                append("MX: 3\r\n")
                append("ST: $SEARCH_TARGET\r\n")
                append("\r\n")
            }

            val socket = DatagramSocket()
            socket.soTimeout = timeoutMs

            val sendData = searchMessage.toByteArray()
            val group = InetAddress.getByName(SSDP_IP)
            val packet = DatagramPacket(sendData, sendData.size, group, SSDP_PORT)
            socket.send(packet)

            val receiveBuffer = ByteArray(4096)
            val startTime = System.currentTimeMillis()

            while (System.currentTimeMillis() - startTime < timeoutMs) {
                try {
                    val receivePacket = DatagramPacket(receiveBuffer, receiveBuffer.size)
                    socket.receive(receivePacket)
                    val response = String(receivePacket.data, 0, receivePacket.length)

                    val location = extractHeader(response, "LOCATION")
                    val usn = extractHeader(response, "USN") ?: location

                    if (location != null && !discoveredMap.containsKey(location)) {
                        val device = UpnpDeviceParser.fetchAndParse(location)
                        if (device != null) {
                            discoveredMap[location] = device
                            emit(discoveredMap.values.toList())
                        }
                    }
                } catch (e: SocketTimeoutException) {
                    break
                } catch (e: Exception) {
                    Log.w(TAG, "Error receiving SSDP packet", e)
                }
            }
            socket.close()
        } finally {
            if (multicastLock.isHeld) {
                multicastLock.release()
            }
        }
        emit(discoveredMap.values.toList())
    }.flowOn(Dispatchers.IO)

    private fun extractHeader(response: String, headerName: String): String? {
        val lines = response.split("\r?\n".toRegex())
        for (line in lines) {
            val parts = line.split(":", limit = 2)
            if (parts.size == 2 && parts[0].trim().equals(headerName, ignoreCase = true)) {
                return parts[1].trim()
            }
        }
        return null
    }
}

[ФАЙЛ 37: app/src/main/java/com/replayer/network/upnp/UpnpDeviceParser.kt] (98 lines)
================================================================================
package com.replayer.network.upnp

import android.util.Log
import android.util.Xml
import com.replayer.model.UpnpDevice
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext
import okhttp3.OkHttpClient
import okhttp3.Request
import org.xmlpull.v1.XmlPullParser
import java.io.StringReader
import java.net.URL
import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit

object UpnpDeviceParser {
    private const val TAG = "UpnpDeviceParser"
    private val client = OkHttpClient.Builder()
        .connectTimeout(3, TimeUnit.SECONDS)
        .readTimeout(3, TimeUnit.SECONDS)
        .build()

    suspend fun fetchAndParse(locationUrl: String): UpnpDevice? = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        try {
            val request = Request.Builder().url(locationUrl).build()
            val response = client.newCall(request).execute()
            if (!response.isSuccessful) return@withContext null
            val body = response.body?.string() ?: return@withContext null

            val parser = Xml.newPullParser()
            parser.setInput(StringReader(body))

            var friendlyName = "DLNA Media Server"
            var manufacturer = "Unknown"
            var udn = locationUrl
            var contentDirectoryControlUrl: String? = null
            var insideContentDirectory = false
            var currentTag = ""

            var eventType = parser.eventType
            while (eventType != XmlPullParser.END_DOCUMENT) {
                when (eventType) {
                    XmlPullParser.START_TAG -> {
                        currentTag = parser.name
                        if (currentTag == "service") {
                            insideContentDirectory = false
                        }
                    }
                    XmlPullParser.TEXT -> {
                        val text = parser.text.trim()
                        if (text.isNotEmpty()) {
                            when (currentTag) {
                                "friendlyName" -> friendlyName = text
                                "manufacturer" -> manufacturer = text
                                "UDN" -> udn = text
                                "serviceType" -> {
                                    if (text.contains("ContentDirectory")) {
                                        insideContentDirectory = true
                                    }
                                }
                                "controlURL" -> {
                                    if (insideContentDirectory && contentDirectoryControlUrl == null) {
                                        contentDirectoryControlUrl = resolveUrl(locationUrl, text)
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    XmlPullParser.END_TAG -> {
                        if (parser.name == "service") {
                            insideContentDirectory = false
                        }
                    }
                }
                eventType = parser.next()
            }

            UpnpDevice(
                id = udn,
                friendlyName = friendlyName,
                manufacturer = manufacturer,
                location = locationUrl,
                contentDirectoryControlUrl = contentDirectoryControlUrl
            )
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Error parsing device description for $locationUrl", e)
            null
        }
    }

    fun resolveUrl(baseUrl: String, path: String): String {
        return if (path.startsWith("http://") || path.startsWith("https://")) {
            path
        } else {
            val base = URL(baseUrl)
            URL(base, path).toString()
        }
    }
}

[ФАЙЛ 38: app/src/main/java/com/replayer/player/RePlayerEngine.kt] (346 lines)
