@Composable
fun HomeScreen(
    onOpenFavorite: (FavoriteFolder) -> Unit,
    onPlayMedia: (MediaItem) -> Unit,
    onPlayChannel: (IptvChannel) -> Unit,
    enterSectionTrigger: Int = 0,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
) {
    val context = LocalContext.current
    val favoritesManager = remember { FavoritesManager(context) }
    val recentManager = remember { RecentMediaManager.getInstance(context) }
    val iptvManager = remember { IptvManager.getInstance(context) }
    val serverConfigManager = remember { ServerConfigManager(context) }

    val favorites by favoritesManager.favorites.collectAsState()
    val recentList by recentManager.recentList.collectAsState()
    val progressMap by recentManager.progressMap.collectAsState()
    val connectedDevice by serverConfigManager.connectedUpnpDevice.collectAsState()
    val channels by iptvManager.channels.collectAsState()
    val favoriteChannelIds by iptvManager.favoriteChannelIds.collectAsState()
    val watchTimes by iptvManager.watchTimes.collectAsState()
    val epgMap by iptvManager.epgMap.collectAsState()

    val favoriteChannels = remember(channels, favoriteChannelIds, watchTimes) {
        iptvManager.getFavoriteChannels()
    }
    val entryFocusRequester = remember { FocusRequester() }

    LaunchedEffect(enterSectionTrigger) {
        if (enterSectionTrigger > 0) {
            delay(50)
            try {
                entryFocusRequester.requestFocus()
            } catch (_: Exception) {}
        }
    }

    LaunchedEffect(connectedDevice?.contentDirectoryControlUrl, recentList) {
        if (recentList.any { it.thumbnailUrl.isNullOrBlank() }) {
            recentManager.resolveMissingPosters(connectedDevice?.contentDirectoryControlUrl)
        }
    }

    var nowMs by remember { mutableLongStateOf(System.currentTimeMillis()) }
    LaunchedEffect(Unit) {
        while (isActive) {
            delay(30_000L)
            nowMs = System.currentTimeMillis()
        }
    }

    Column(
        modifier = modifier
            .fillMaxSize()
            .verticalScroll(rememberScrollState())
            .padding(start = 28.dp, end = 28.dp, top = 34.dp, bottom = 18.dp)
    ) {
        // -------------------------------------------------------------
        // ВЕРХНИЙ БЛОК: СЛЕВА ПОСТЕРЫ (~ПОЛОВИНА ЭКРАНА) | СПРАВА «ИЗБРАННЫЕ ПАПКИ» ДО КРАЯ ЭКРАНА
        // -------------------------------------------------------------
        val posterSectionHeight = 208.dp
        val recentPosters = remember(recentList) { recentList.take(2) }
        Row(
            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
            verticalAlignment = Alignment.Top
        ) {
            // LEFT COLUMN: Продолжить просмотр (~46% ширины, до 2 постеров с одной папки макс.)
            Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(0.46f)) {
                Text(
                    text = "Продолжить просмотр",
                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium.copy(
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                        fontSize = 17.sp
                    ),
                    color = TextPrimary,
                    maxLines = 1
                )

                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(10.dp))

                if (recentPosters.isEmpty()) {
                    Box(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .height(posterSectionHeight)
                            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(12.dp))
                            .background(DarkCard)
                            .padding(16.dp),
                        contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                    ) {
                        Column(horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally) {
                            Icon(
                                imageVector = Icons.Outlined.PlayCircle,
                                contentDescription = null,
                                tint = AccentCyan.copy(alpha = 0.55f),
                                modifier = Modifier.size(34.dp)
                            )
                            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(6.dp))
                            Text(
                                text = "Нет недавних видео",
                                style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                                color = TextSecondary
                            )
                        }
                    }
                } else {
                    Row(
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(14.dp)
                    ) {
                        recentPosters.forEachIndexed { idx, recent ->
                            val watchProgress = remember(recent, progressMap) {
                                (progressMap[recent.id]?.progress()
                                    ?: progressMap[recent.uri]?.progress()
                                    ?: recent.progress()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
                            }

                            HomeShelfFocusCard(
                                onClick = { onPlayMedia(recent.toMediaItem()) },
                                modifier = Modifier
                                    .weight(1f)
                                    .height(posterSectionHeight)
                                    .then(if (idx == 0) Modifier.focusRequester(entryFocusRequester) else Modifier)
                            ) { isFocused ->
                                Box(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
                                    // Background: Full-bleed vertical cover poster OR sleek slate fallback
                                    if (!recent.thumbnailUrl.isNullOrBlank()) {
                                        AsyncImage(
                                            model = recent.thumbnailUrl,
                                            contentDescription = recent.title,
                                            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
                                            contentScale = ContentScale.Crop
                                        )
                                    } else {
                                        Box(
                                            modifier = Modifier
                                                .fillMaxSize()
                                                .background(
                                                    Brush.verticalGradient(
                                                        colors = listOf(
                                                            Color(0xFF222A36),
                                                            Color(0xFF141820)
                                                        )
                                                    )
                                                )
                                        )
                                        // Subtle gradient watch fill across fallback poster background
                                        if (watchProgress > 0f) {
                                            Box(
                                                modifier = Modifier
                                                    .fillMaxHeight()
                                                    .fillMaxWidth(fraction = watchProgress)
                                                    .background(
                                                        Brush.horizontalGradient(
                                                            colors = listOf(
                                                                AccentCyan.copy(alpha = 0.06f),
                                                                AccentCyan.copy(alpha = 0.20f)
                                                            )
                                                        )
                                                    )
                                            )
                                        }
                                    }

                                    // Bottom readability scrim
                                    Box(
                                        modifier = Modifier
                                            .fillMaxWidth()
                                            .fillMaxHeight(0.50f)
                                            .align(Alignment.BottomCenter)
                                            .background(
                                                Brush.verticalGradient(
                                                    colors = listOf(
                                                        Color.Transparent,
                                                        Color(0xE60B0E14)
                                                    )
                                                )
                                            )
                                    )

                                    // Single-line title with marquee on focus
                                    Column(
                                        modifier = Modifier
                                            .fillMaxWidth()
                                            .align(Alignment.BottomCenter)
                                    ) {
                                        Text(
                                            text = recent.title,
                                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall.copy(
                                                fontSize = 13.sp,
                                                fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold
                                            ),
                                            color = TextPrimary,
                                            maxLines = 1,
                                            overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis,
                                            modifier = Modifier
                                                .fillMaxWidth()
                                                .padding(start = 10.dp, end = 10.dp, top = 6.dp, bottom = 5.dp)
                                                .then(
                                                    if (isFocused) {
                                                        Modifier.basicMarquee(
                                                            iterations = Int.MAX_VALUE,
                                                            initialDelayMillis = 250,
                                                            repeatDelayMillis = 800,
                                                            velocity = 42.dp
                                                        )
                                                    } else Modifier
                                                )
                                        )

                                        // Raised high-contrast watch progress pill (Amber-Orange vs Cyan FocusBorder)
                                        Box(
                                            modifier = Modifier
                                                .fillMaxWidth()
                                                .padding(start = 10.dp, end = 10.dp, bottom = 9.dp)
                                                .height(5.dp)
                                                .clip(RoundedCornerShape(3.dp))
                                                .background(Color(0xD907090E))
                                                .border(0.5.dp, Color(0x40FFFFFF), RoundedCornerShape(3.dp))
                                        ) {
                                            if (watchProgress > 0f) {
                                                Box(
                                                    modifier = Modifier
                                                        .fillMaxHeight()
                                                        .fillMaxWidth(fraction = watchProgress)
                                                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(3.dp))
                                                        .background(
                                                            Brush.horizontalGradient(
                                                                colors = listOf(
                                                                    AccentGold,
                                                                    AccentOrange
                                                                )
                                                            )
                                                        )
                                                )
                                            }
                                        }
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        }

                        // Preserve crisp vertical poster proportions when only 1 recent item exists
                        if (recentPosters.size == 1) {
                            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f))
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(20.dp))

            // RIGHT COLUMN: Избранные папки DLNA (растянуты до правого края экрана)
            Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(0.54f)) {
                Text(
                    text = "Избранные папки",
                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium.copy(
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                        fontSize = 17.sp
                    ),
                    color = TextPrimary,
                    maxLines = 1
                )

                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(10.dp))

                if (favorites.isEmpty()) {
                    Box(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .height(posterSectionHeight)
                            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(12.dp))
                            .background(DarkCard)
                            .padding(horizontal = 24.dp, vertical = 20.dp),
                        contentAlignment = Alignment.CenterStart
                    ) {
                        Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                            Icon(
                                imageVector = Icons.Outlined.FolderOpen,
                                contentDescription = null,
                                tint = TextSecondary,
                                modifier = Modifier.size(26.dp)
                            )
                            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(12.dp))
                            Text(
                                text = "Нажимайте звёздочку ★ рядом с папкой в DLNA для быстрого доступа",
                                style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium,
                                color = TextSecondary
                            )
                        }
                    }
                } else {
                    BoxWithConstraints(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .height(posterSectionHeight)
                    ) {
                        val gridWidth = maxWidth
                        val cardWidth = if (favorites.size <= 2) {
                            gridWidth - 4.dp
                        } else {
                            ((gridWidth - 16.dp) / 2f).coerceAtLeast(200.dp)
                        }

                        LazyHorizontalGrid(
                            rows = GridCells.Fixed(2),
                            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp),
                            verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp),
                            contentPadding = PaddingValues(horizontal = 2.dp, vertical = 2.dp),
                            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
                        ) {
                            gridItemsIndexed(favorites, key = { idx, fav -> "${fav.id}_$idx" }) { idx, fav ->
                                HomeShelfFocusCard(
                                    onClick = { onOpenFavorite(fav) },
                                    modifier = Modifier
                                        .width(cardWidth)
                                        .fillMaxHeight()
                                        .then(
                                            if (recentPosters.isEmpty() && idx == 0) {
                                                Modifier.focusRequester(entryFocusRequester)
                                            } else Modifier
                                        )
                                ) { _ ->
                                    Row(
                                        modifier = Modifier
                                            .fillMaxSize()
                                            .padding(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = 12.dp),
                                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                                    ) {
                                        Row(
                                            modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
                                            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                                        ) {
                                            Box(
                                                modifier = Modifier
                                                    .size(40.dp)
                                                    .clip(RoundedCornerShape(8.dp))
                                                    .background(AccentCyan.copy(alpha = 0.14f)),
                                                contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                                            ) {
                                                Icon(
                                                    imageVector = Icons.Outlined.FolderOpen,
                                                    contentDescription = null,
                                                    tint = AccentCyan,
                                                    modifier = Modifier.size(21.dp)
                                                )
                                            }
                                            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(12.dp))
                                            Text(
                                                text = fav.title,
                                                style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleSmall.copy(
                                                    fontSize = 14.sp,
                                                    fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold,
                                                    lineHeight = 18.sp
                                                ),
                                                color = TextPrimary,
                                                maxLines = 2,
                                                overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis
                                            )
                                        }
                                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(8.dp))
                                        Icon(
                                            imageVector = Icons.Rounded.Star,
                                            contentDescription = "В избранном",
                                            tint = AccentGold,
                                            modifier = Modifier.size(18.dp)
                                        )
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(20.dp))

        // -------------------------------------------------------------
        // НИЖНИЙ БЛОК: ТЕЛЕПРОГРАММА ИЗБРАННЫХ КАНАЛОВ (Google TV Live Guide)
        // -------------------------------------------------------------
        Text(
            text = "Телепрограмма избранных каналов",
            style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium.copy(
                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                fontSize = 17.sp
            ),
            color = TextPrimary
        )

        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(10.dp))

        if (favoriteChannels.isEmpty()) {
            Box(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxWidth()
                    .clip(RoundedCornerShape(12.dp))
                    .background(DarkCard)
                    .padding(horizontal = 20.dp, vertical = 20.dp),
                contentAlignment = Alignment.CenterStart
            ) {
                Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                    Icon(
                        imageVector = Icons.Outlined.LiveTv,
                        contentDescription = null,
                        tint = TextSecondary,
                        modifier = Modifier.size(24.dp)
                    )
                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(12.dp))
                    Text(
                        text = "Отмечайте каналы звёздочкой в разделе «ТВ каналы», чтобы видеть их программу передач здесь.",
                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium,
                        color = TextSecondary
                    )
                }
            }
        } else {
            GoogleTvLiveGuide(
                favoriteChannels = favoriteChannels,
                epgMap = epgMap,
                iptvManager = iptvManager,
                nowMs = nowMs,
                entryFocusRequester = if (recentPosters.isEmpty() && favorites.isEmpty()) entryFocusRequester else null,
                onPlayChannel = onPlayChannel
            )
        }
    }
}

private data class TimelineSlot(
    val program: EpgProgram?,
    val startMs: Long,
    val endMs: Long,
    val startedBeforeWindow: Boolean
)

private fun buildTimelineSlots(
    programs: List<EpgProgram>,
    windowStart: Long,
    windowEnd: Long
): List<TimelineSlot> {
    val overlapping = programs
        .filter { it.endTime > windowStart && it.startTime < windowEnd }
        .sortedBy { it.startTime }

    if (overlapping.isEmpty()) {
        return listOf(TimelineSlot(null, windowStart, windowEnd, false))
    }

    val rawSlots = mutableListOf<TimelineSlot>()
    var cursor = windowStart
    val minGapMs = 4 * 60_000L

    for (prog in overlapping) {
        val clippedStart = maxOf(prog.startTime, windowStart)
        val clippedEnd = minOf(prog.endTime, windowEnd)
        if (clippedEnd <= clippedStart) continue

        if (clippedStart - cursor >= minGapMs) {
            rawSlots.add(TimelineSlot(null, cursor, clippedStart, false))
        }
        val actualStart = if (clippedStart - cursor < minGapMs && rawSlots.isEmpty()) windowStart else clippedStart
        rawSlots.add(
            TimelineSlot(
                program = prog,
                startMs = actualStart,
                endMs = clippedEnd,
                startedBeforeWindow = prog.startTime < windowStart
            )
        )
        cursor = maxOf(cursor, clippedEnd)
    }

    if (windowEnd - cursor >= minGapMs) {
        rawSlots.add(TimelineSlot(null, cursor, windowEnd, false))
    } else if (rawSlots.isNotEmpty()) {
        val last = rawSlots.removeAt(rawSlots.lastIndex)
        rawSlots.add(last.copy(endMs = windowEnd))
    }

    return if (rawSlots.isEmpty()) {
        listOf(TimelineSlot(null, windowStart, windowEnd, false))
    } else {
        rawSlots
    }
}

@Composable
private fun GoogleTvLiveGuide(
    favoriteChannels: List<IptvChannel>,
    epgMap: Map<String, List<EpgProgram>>,
    iptvManager: IptvManager,
    nowMs: Long,
    entryFocusRequester: FocusRequester? = null,
    onPlayChannel: (IptvChannel) -> Unit
) {
    val totalWindowMs = 75 * 60_000L // 1h 15m visible window matching Google TV
    val windowStart = nowMs - 15 * 60_000L // NOW sits cleanly at 20%
    val windowEnd = windowStart + totalWindowMs
    val nowFraction = 0.20f

    val dateStr = remember(nowMs) {
        SimpleDateFormat("EE, dd.MM", Locale("ru")).format(Date(nowMs)).uppercase()
    }
    val nowTimeStr = remember(nowMs) {
        SimpleDateFormat("HH:mm", Locale.getDefault()).format(Date(nowMs))
    }
    val halfHourTicks = remember(nowMs) {
        val step = 30 * 60_000L
        val firstTick = ((windowStart / step) + 1) * step
        val ticks = mutableListOf<Pair<Float, String>>()
        val fmt = SimpleDateFormat("HH:mm", Locale.getDefault())
        var t = firstTick
        while (t < windowEnd - 6 * 60_000L) {
            if (abs(t - nowMs) >= 7 * 60_000L && (t - windowStart) >= 5 * 60_000L) {
                val frac = (t - windowStart).toFloat() / totalWindowMs.toFloat()
                ticks.add(frac to fmt.format(Date(t)))
            }
            t += step
        }
        ticks
    }

    val logoColWidth = 112.dp
    val colGap = 10.dp

    Box(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth()) {
        Column(
            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
            verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(4.dp)
        ) {
            // Timeline Ruler Header
            Row(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxWidth()
                    .padding(horizontal = 4.dp)
                    .height(26.dp),
                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
            ) {
                Row(
                    modifier = Modifier.width(logoColWidth),
                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                ) {
                    Text(
                        text = dateStr,
                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall.copy(fontSize = 11.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Medium),
                        color = TextSecondary,
                        maxLines = 1
                    )
                    Text(
                        text = "В ЭФИРЕ",
                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall.copy(fontSize = 10.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold),
                        color = TextSecondary,
                        maxLines = 1
                    )
                }

                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(colGap))

                BoxWithConstraints(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .weight(1f)
                        .fillMaxHeight()
                ) {
                    val widthDp = maxWidth
                    // Current Amber-Orange time label right next to the live dot
                    Text(
                        text = nowTimeStr,
                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall.copy(fontSize = 11.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold),
                        color = AccentOrange,
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .offset(x = widthDp * nowFraction + 8.dp)
                            .align(Alignment.CenterStart)
                    )

                    // 30-min grid markers (16:00, 16:30, ...)
                    halfHourTicks.forEach { (frac, label) ->
                        Text(
                            text = label,
                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall.copy(fontSize = 11.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Medium),
                            color = TextSecondary,
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .offset(x = widthDp * frac)
                                .align(Alignment.CenterStart)
                        )
                    }
                }
            }

            // Channel Rows (single focusable unit per channel row)
            favoriteChannels.forEachIndexed { channelIdx, channel ->
                val progs = remember(channel, epgMap) {
                    iptvManager.getProgramsForChannel(channel)
                }
                val slots = remember(progs, windowStart, windowEnd) {
                    buildTimelineSlots(progs, windowStart, windowEnd)
                }
                val rowInteractionSource = remember { MutableInteractionSource() }
                val isRowFocused by rowInteractionSource.collectIsFocusedAsState()
                val rowScale by animateFloatAsState(
                    targetValue = if (isRowFocused) 1.012f else 1.0f,
                    label = "gtv_row_scale"
                )

                Row(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .scale(rowScale)
                        .zIndex(if (isRowFocused) 1f else 0f)
                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(14.dp))
                        .background(if (isRowFocused) CardFocusedBackground else Color.Transparent)
                        .border(
                            width = if (isRowFocused) 2.dp else 1.dp,
                            color = if (isRowFocused) FocusBorder else Color.Transparent,
                            shape = RoundedCornerShape(14.dp)
                        )
                        .then(
                            if (channelIdx == 0 && entryFocusRequester != null) {
                                Modifier.focusRequester(entryFocusRequester)
                            } else Modifier
                        )
                        .clickable(
                            interactionSource = rowInteractionSource,
                            indication = null,
                            onClick = { onPlayChannel(channel) }
                        )
                        .focusable(interactionSource = rowInteractionSource)
                        .padding(horizontal = 4.dp, vertical = 3.dp)
                        .height(56.dp),
                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                ) {
                    // Left Channel Logo Box (clean logo only, no star badge)
                    Box(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .width(logoColWidth)
                            .fillMaxHeight()
                            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(10.dp))
                            .background(if (isRowFocused) Color(0xFF242934) else Color(0xFF191C22))
                            .border(
                                width = 1.dp,
                                color = if (isRowFocused) Color(0x66FFFFFF) else Color(0xFF262B35),
                                shape = RoundedCornerShape(10.dp)
                            ),
                        contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                    ) {
                        if (!channel.logoUrl.isNullOrBlank()) {
                            AsyncImage(
                                model = channel.logoUrl,
                                contentDescription = channel.name,
                                modifier = Modifier
                                    .fillMaxSize()
                                    .padding(horizontal = 12.dp, vertical = 8.dp),
                                contentScale = ContentScale.Fit
                            )
                        } else {
                            Text(
                                text = channel.name,
                                style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelMedium.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, fontSize = 11.sp),
                                color = TextPrimary,
                                maxLines = 2,
                                overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis,
                                modifier = Modifier.padding(horizontal = 8.dp)
                            )
                        }
                    }

                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(colGap))

                    // Right Timeline Slots (purely visual inside focused channel row)
                    Row(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .weight(1f)
                            .fillMaxHeight(),
                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(6.dp)
                    ) {
                        slots.forEach { slot ->
                            val durationMs = (slot.endMs - slot.startMs).coerceAtLeast(60_000L)
                            val weight = (durationMs.toFloat() / totalWindowMs.toFloat()).coerceAtLeast(0.05f)
                            val isTooNarrowForText = durationMs < 6 * 60_000L
                            val isLiveSlot = nowMs in slot.startMs until slot.endMs
                            val title = if (slot.program != null) {
                                if (slot.startedBeforeWindow) "‹  ${slot.program.title}" else slot.program.title
                            } else {
                                "Прямой эфир"
                            }

                            GoogleTvProgramTile(
                                title = if (isTooNarrowForText) "" else title,
                                isHighlighted = isRowFocused && isLiveSlot,
                                isRowFocused = isRowFocused,
                                modifier = Modifier
                                    .weight(weight)
                                    .fillMaxHeight()
                            )
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        // Amber-Orange vertical NOW indicator line across the entire timeline
        Row(
            modifier = Modifier
                .matchParentSize()
                .padding(top = 9.dp, start = 4.dp, end = 4.dp)
        ) {
            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(logoColWidth + colGap))
            BoxWithConstraints(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .weight(1f)
                    .fillMaxHeight()
            ) {
                val nowX = maxWidth * nowFraction
                // Top Amber Circle Dot
                Box(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .offset(x = nowX - 3.5.dp, y = 0.dp)
                        .size(7.dp)
                        .clip(CircleShape)
                        .background(AccentOrange)
                )
                // Continuous Vertical Amber-Orange Line
                Box(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .offset(x = nowX - 0.75.dp, y = 4.dp)
                        .width(1.5.dp)
                        .fillMaxHeight()
                        .background(
                            Brush.verticalGradient(
                                colors = listOf(
                                    AccentOrange,
                                    AccentOrange.copy(alpha = 0.85f),
                                    AccentOrange.copy(alpha = 0.2f)
                                )
                            )
                        )
                )
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun GoogleTvProgramTile(
    title: String,
    isHighlighted: Boolean,
    isRowFocused: Boolean,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
) {
    val bgColor = when {
        isHighlighted -> Color(0xFFE8EAED)
        isRowFocused -> Color(0xFF232732)
        else -> Color(0xFF1A1D24)
    }
    val borderColor = when {
        isHighlighted -> Color.White
        isRowFocused -> Color(0xFF3E4554)
        else -> Color(0xFF282C36)
    }
    val textColor = if (isHighlighted) Color(0xFF111318) else TextPrimary

    Box(
        modifier = modifier
            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(10.dp))
            .background(bgColor)
            .border(1.dp, borderColor, RoundedCornerShape(10.dp))
            .padding(horizontal = 14.dp),
        contentAlignment = Alignment.CenterStart
    ) {
        if (title.isNotEmpty()) {
            Text(
                text = title,
                style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium.copy(
                    fontSize = 14.sp,
                    fontWeight = if (isHighlighted) FontWeight.Bold else FontWeight.Medium
                ),
                color = textColor,
                maxLines = 1,
                overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis
            )
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun HomeShelfFocusCard(
    onClick: () -> Unit,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    content: @Composable BoxScope.(isFocused: Boolean) -> Unit
) {
    val interactionSource = remember { MutableInteractionSource() }
    val isFocused by interactionSource.collectIsFocusedAsState()
    val scale by animateFloatAsState(if (isFocused) 1.03f else 1.0f, label = "shelf_card_scale")

    Box(
        modifier = modifier
            .padding(3.dp)
            .zIndex(if (isFocused) 10f else 1f)
            .scale(scale)
            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(14.dp))
            .background(if (isFocused) CardFocusedBackground else CardBackground)
            .then(
                if (isFocused) {
                    Modifier.border(2.5.dp, FocusBorder, RoundedCornerShape(14.dp))
                } else {
                    Modifier.border(1.dp, BorderSubtle, RoundedCornerShape(14.dp))
                }
            )
            .clickable(
                interactionSource = interactionSource,
                indication = null,
                onClick = onClick
            )
            .focusable(interactionSource = interactionSource)
    ) {
        content(isFocused)
    }
}

package com.replayer.ui.screens

import androidx.activity.compose.BackHandler
import androidx.compose.animation.animateColorAsState
import androidx.compose.animation.core.animateFloatAsState
import androidx.compose.foundation.background
import androidx.compose.foundation.border
import androidx.compose.foundation.clickable
import androidx.compose.foundation.focusable
import androidx.compose.foundation.interaction.MutableInteractionSource
import androidx.compose.foundation.interaction.collectIsFocusedAsState
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.*
import androidx.compose.foundation.lazy.LazyColumn
import androidx.compose.foundation.lazy.grid.GridCells
import androidx.compose.foundation.lazy.grid.LazyVerticalGrid
import androidx.compose.foundation.lazy.grid.items
import androidx.compose.foundation.lazy.grid.itemsIndexed

import androidx.compose.ui.focus.focusRequester

import androidx.compose.ui.unit.sp

import coil.compose.AsyncImage
import com.replayer.data.ChannelSortMode
import com.replayer.data.IptvManager
import com.replayer.model.IptvChannel
import com.replayer.ui.components.TvFocusCard
import com.replayer.ui.theme.*
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.delay
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
import java.io.File

@Composable
fun IptvScreen(
    onPlayChannel: (IptvChannel) -> Unit,
    onNavigateToSettings: () -> Unit,
    onBackToHome: () -> Unit = {},
    enterSectionTrigger: Int = 0,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
) {
    val context = LocalContext.current
    val scope = rememberCoroutineScope()
    val iptvManager = remember { IptvManager.getInstance(context) }

    val channels by iptvManager.channels.collectAsState()
    val favoriteIds by iptvManager.favoriteChannelIds.collectAsState()
    val epgMap by iptvManager.epgMap.collectAsState()
    val watchTimes by iptvManager.watchTimes.collectAsState()
    val isLoading by iptvManager.isLoading.collectAsState()
    val epgProgress by iptvManager.epgProgress.collectAsState()
    val statusMsg by iptvManager.statusMessage.collectAsState()

    // Level 1: Category folder selection. Level 2: Inside folder
    var selectedFolder by remember { mutableStateOf<String?>(null) }
    var sortMode by remember { mutableStateOf(ChannelSortMode.BY_WATCH_TIME) }
    val entryFocusRequester = remember { FocusRequester() }
    val holdFocusRequester = remember { FocusRequester() }
    var isSwitchingView by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var pendingFolderTarget by remember { mutableStateOf<String?>(null) }
    var pendingHasSwitch by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }

    fun switchCategory(targetFolder: String?) {
        pendingFolderTarget = targetFolder
        pendingHasSwitch = true
        isSwitchingView = true
    }

    LaunchedEffect(isSwitchingView, pendingHasSwitch) {
        if (!isSwitchingView || !pendingHasSwitch) return@LaunchedEffect
        delay(16)
        try {
            holdFocusRequester.requestFocus()
        } catch (_: Exception) {}
        selectedFolder = pendingFolderTarget
        pendingHasSwitch = false
        delay(45)
        try {
            entryFocusRequester.requestFocus()
        } catch (_: Exception) {}
        isSwitchingView = false
    }

    LaunchedEffect(enterSectionTrigger, isLoading, channels.isEmpty()) {
        if (enterSectionTrigger > 0 && !isSwitchingView) {
            delay(50)
            try {
                entryFocusRequester.requestFocus()
            } catch (_: Exception) {}
        }
    }

    val folders = remember(channels) { iptvManager.getCategoryFolders() }

    var showLocalFileDialog by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var localFiles by remember { mutableStateOf<List<File>>(emptyList()) }
    var isScanningLocalFiles by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }

    fun scanLocalFiles() {
        isScanningLocalFiles = true
        scope.launch(Dispatchers.IO) {
            val candidates = mutableListOf<File>()
            val searchDirs = listOfNotNull(
                File("/storage"),
                File("/sdcard"),
                File("/storage/emulated/0/Download"),
                File("/storage/emulated/0"),
                context.getExternalFilesDir(null)
            )
            for (dir in searchDirs) {
                try {
                    if (dir.exists() && dir.canRead()) {
                        dir.walkTopDown().maxDepth(3).forEach { f ->
                            if (f.isFile && (f.name.endsWith(".m3u", ignoreCase = true) || f.name.endsWith(".m3u8", ignoreCase = true))) {
                                candidates.add(f)
                            }
                        }
                    }
                } catch (_: Exception) {}
            }
            localFiles = candidates.distinctBy { it.absolutePath }
            isScanningLocalFiles = false
            showLocalFileDialog = true
        }
    }

    // Remote back button handling: inside folder -> back to folders; on folders -> back to Home
    BackHandler(enabled = true) {
        if (selectedFolder != null && folders.size > 1) {
            switchCategory(null)
        } else {
            onBackToHome()
        }
    }

    val isInsideFolder = selectedFolder != null || (folders.size <= 1 && channels.isNotEmpty())

    Column(
        modifier = modifier
            .fillMaxSize()
            .padding(start = 28.dp, end = 28.dp, top = 24.dp, bottom = 16.dp)
    ) {
        if (isSwitchingView) {
            Box(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .size(1.dp)
                    .focusRequester(holdFocusRequester)
                    .focusable()
            )
        }
        // TOP BAR — vertically aligned with the left 64dp Logo
        Row(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxWidth()
                .heightIn(min = 64.dp),
            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
        ) {
            if (isInsideFolder) {
                val currentFolder = selectedFolder ?: folders.firstOrNull() ?: "Все каналы"
                // Inside Folder Header (Compact so 8+ channels fit)
                Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                    if (folders.size > 1 && selectedFolder != null) {
                        TvFocusCard(
                            onClick = { switchCategory(null) },
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .width(116.dp)
                                .height(38.dp)
                        ) {
                            Row(
                                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
                                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.Center,
                                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                            ) {
                                Icon(Icons.Rounded.ArrowBackIosNew, contentDescription = "Назад", tint = AccentCyan, modifier = Modifier.size(14.dp))
                                Spacer(Modifier.width(6.dp))
                                Text("Папки", style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold), color = AccentCyan)
                            }
                        }
                        Spacer(Modifier.width(14.dp))
                    }
                    Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                        Text(
                            text = if (folders.size <= 1) "ТВ каналы" else currentFolder,
                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleLarge.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold),
                            color = TextPrimary
                        )
                        val folderChannels = remember(channels, currentFolder) {
                            iptvManager.getChannelsInFolder(currentFolder)
                        }
                        Spacer(Modifier.width(12.dp))
                        Text(
                            text = "• ${folderChannels.size} каналов",
                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                            color = TextSecondary
                        )
                    }
                }

                // Sort toggle button inside folder
                TvFocusCard(
                    onClick = {
                        sortMode = when (sortMode) {
                            ChannelSortMode.BY_WATCH_TIME -> ChannelSortMode.BY_NAME
                            ChannelSortMode.BY_NAME -> ChannelSortMode.DEFAULT
                            ChannelSortMode.DEFAULT -> ChannelSortMode.BY_WATCH_TIME
                        }
                    },
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .width(210.dp)
                        .height(38.dp)
                ) {
                    Row(
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.Center,
                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                    ) {
                        Icon(Icons.Outlined.SwapVert, contentDescription = null, tint = AccentCyan, modifier = Modifier.size(16.dp))
                        Spacer(Modifier.width(6.dp))
                        Text(text = sortMode.label, style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall, color = AccentCyan)
                    }
                }
            } else {
                // Top Level (Folders List) Header
                Column {
                    Text(
                        text = "ТВ каналы",
                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.displayLarge
                    )
                    Text(
                        text = if (channels.isEmpty()) "Плейлист не настроен" else "Категорий: ${folders.size} • Всего каналов: ${channels.size}",
                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium,
                        color = TextSecondary,
                        modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 2.dp)
                    )
                }

                Row(horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp)) {
                    if (channels.isNotEmpty()) {
                        TvFocusCard(
                            onClick = { scope.launch { iptvManager.refreshPlaylist() } },
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .width(150.dp)
                                .height(44.dp)
                        ) {
                            Row(
                                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
                                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.Center,
                                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                            ) {
                                Icon(Icons.Outlined.Refresh, contentDescription = null, tint = AccentCyan, modifier = Modifier.size(18.dp))
                                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(6.dp))
                                Text("Обновить", style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium)
                            }
                        }
                    }

                    TvFocusCard(
                        onClick = onNavigateToSettings,
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .width(160.dp)
                            .height(44.dp)
                            .then(
                                if (channels.isEmpty() && isLoading) Modifier.focusRequester(entryFocusRequester)
                                else Modifier
                            )
                    ) {
                        Row(
                            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
                            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.Center,
                            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                        ) {
                            Icon(Icons.Outlined.Settings, contentDescription = null, tint = AccentCyan, modifier = Modifier.size(18.dp))
                            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(6.dp))
                            Text("Настройки ТВ", style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium)
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        // Live EPG / Playlist background progress indicator (Requirement 1)
        if (epgProgress != null || isLoading) {
            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(10.dp))
            Row(
                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxWidth()
                    .clip(RoundedCornerShape(10.dp))
                    .background(DarkCard)
                    .border(1.dp, BorderSubtle, RoundedCornerShape(10.dp))
                    .padding(horizontal = 14.dp, vertical = 8.dp)
            ) {
                Box(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .weight(1f)
                        .height(6.dp)
                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(3.dp))
                        .background(DarkSurface)
                ) {
                    Box(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth(fraction = (epgProgress ?: 0.5f).coerceIn(0.04f, 1f))
                            .fillMaxHeight()
                            .background(
                                Brush.horizontalGradient(
                                    colors = listOf(AccentCyan, AccentGold, AccentOrange)
                                )
                            )
                    )
                }
                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(12.dp))
                Text(
                    text = statusMsg ?: "Загрузка...",
                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Medium),
                    color = AccentCyanBright
                )
            }
        }

        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(if (isInsideFolder) 10.dp else 16.dp))

        // MAIN CONTENT
        if (channels.isEmpty()) {
            if (isLoading) {
                Box(
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
                    contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                ) {
                    Row(
                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(14.dp))
                            .background(DarkCard)
                            .border(1.dp, BorderSubtle, RoundedCornerShape(14.dp))
                            .padding(horizontal = 24.dp, vertical = 16.dp)
                    ) {
                        CircularProgressIndicator(
                            color = AccentCyan,
                            strokeWidth = 2.5.dp,
                            modifier = Modifier.size(22.dp)
                        )
                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(14.dp))
                        Text(
                            text = statusMsg ?: "Загрузка каналов...",
                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium,
                            color = TextPrimary
                        )
                    }
                }
            } else {
                // Empty State: Playlist Not Configured
                Box(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxSize()
                        .padding(bottom = 60.dp),
                    contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                ) {
                    Column(
                        horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally,
                        verticalArrangement = Arrangement.Center
                    ) {
                        Box(
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .size(72.dp)
                                .clip(RoundedCornerShape(20.dp))
                                .background(DarkCard),
                            contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                        ) {
                            Icon(
                                imageVector = Icons.Outlined.LiveTv,
                                contentDescription = null,
                                tint = AccentCyan.copy(alpha = 0.7f),
                                modifier = Modifier.size(38.dp)
                            )
                        }
                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(16.dp))
                        Text(
                            text = if (epgMap.isNotEmpty()) "Телепрограмма готова, но нет списка каналов" else "Плейлист IPTV не загружен",
                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleLarge.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold),
                            color = TextPrimary
                        )
                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(8.dp))
                        Text(
                            text = if (epgMap.isNotEmpty()) {
                                "Телепрограмма загружена (${epgMap.size} каналов в базе). Для показа ТВ каналов требуется плейлист (.m3u / .m3u8) со ссылками на видеопотоки."
                            } else {
                                "Добавьте ссылку на .m3u плейлист или выберите файл с флешки"
                            },
                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium,
                            color = TextSecondary,
                            textAlign = TextAlign.Center,
                            modifier = Modifier.padding(horizontal = 48.dp)
                        )
                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(24.dp))
                        Row(horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(16.dp)) {
                            TvFocusCard(
                                onClick = { scanLocalFiles() },
                                modifier = Modifier
                                    .width(240.dp)
                                    .height(48.dp)
                                    .focusRequester(entryFocusRequester)
                            ) {
                                Row(
                                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
                                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.Center,
                                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                                ) {
                                    Icon(Icons.Outlined.FolderOpen, contentDescription = null, tint = AccentCyan, modifier = Modifier.size(20.dp))
                                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(8.dp))
                                    Text(
                                        text = if (isScanningLocalFiles) "Поиск файлов..." else "Плейлист с флешки",
                                        color = AccentCyan,
                                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium
                                    )
                                }
                            }
                            TvFocusCard(
                                onClick = onNavigateToSettings,
                                modifier = Modifier
                                    .width(200.dp)
                                    .height(48.dp)
                            ) {
                                Row(
                                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
                                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.Center,
                                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                                ) {
                                    Icon(Icons.Outlined.Settings, contentDescription = null, tint = TextSecondary, modifier = Modifier.size(20.dp))
                                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(8.dp))
                                    Text("Настройки ТВ", color = TextPrimary, style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium)
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        } else if (selectedFolder == null && folders.size > 1) {
            // LEVEL 1: Category Folders Grid (Requirement 5 & 8)
            LazyVerticalGrid(
                columns = GridCells.Adaptive(minSize = 240.dp),
                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(16.dp),
                verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(16.dp),
                contentPadding = PaddingValues(bottom = 32.dp)
            ) {
                itemsIndexed(folders) { folderIdx, folder ->
                    val count = remember(channels, folder) {
                        iptvManager.getChannelsInFolder(folder).size
                    }

                    TvFocusCard(
                        onClick = { switchCategory(folder) },
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .height(84.dp)
                            .then(if (folderIdx == 0) Modifier.focusRequester(entryFocusRequester) else Modifier)
                    ) {
                        Row(
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .fillMaxSize()
                                .padding(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = 12.dp),
                            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                        ) {
                            Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                                Box(
                                    modifier = Modifier
                                        .size(44.dp)
                                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(12.dp))
                                        .background(AccentCyan.copy(alpha = 0.15f)),
                                    contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                                ) {
                                    Icon(
                                        imageVector = Icons.Outlined.FolderOpen,
                                        contentDescription = null,
                                        tint = AccentCyan,
                                        modifier = Modifier.size(26.dp)
                                    )
                                }
                                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(16.dp))
                                Column {
                                    Text(
                                        text = folder,
                                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold),
                                        color = TextPrimary,
                                        maxLines = 1,
                                        overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis
                                    )
                                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(4.dp))
                                    Text(
                                        text = "$count каналов",
                                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                                        color = TextSecondary
                                    )
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        } else {
            // LEVEL 2: Channels inside the selected folder (or all channels if single folder)
            val currentFolder = selectedFolder ?: folders.firstOrNull() ?: "Все каналы"
            val folderChannels = remember(channels, currentFolder) {
                iptvManager.getChannelsInFolder(currentFolder)
            }
            val sortedList = remember(folderChannels, sortMode, watchTimes) {
                iptvManager.getSortedChannels(folderChannels, sortMode)
            }

            if (sortedList.isEmpty()) {
                Box(
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
                    contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                ) {
                    Text(
                        text = "В этой категории нет каналов",
                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyLarge,
                        color = TextSecondary
                    )
                }
            } else {
                LazyVerticalGrid(
                    columns = GridCells.Fixed(2),
                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp),
                    verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(5.dp),
                    contentPadding = PaddingValues(bottom = 24.dp),
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
                ) {
                    itemsIndexed(sortedList, key = { idx, ch -> "${ch.id}_$idx" }) { idx, channel ->
                        val isFav = favoriteIds.contains(channel.id)
                        val currentProg = remember(channel, epgMap) {
                            iptvManager.getCurrentProgram(channel)
                        }
                        val progress = currentProg?.progress()?.coerceIn(0f, 1f) ?: 0f
                        val starInteraction = remember { MutableInteractionSource() }
                        val isStarFocused by starInteraction.collectIsFocusedAsState()

                        TvFocusCard(
                            onClick = { onPlayChannel(channel) },
                            isSelected = isStarFocused,
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .fillMaxWidth()
                                .height(48.dp)
                                .then(if (idx == 0) Modifier.focusRequester(entryFocusRequester) else Modifier)
                        ) {
                            Box(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
                                // Subtle full-card gradient timebar fill on background
                                if (currentProg != null && progress > 0f) {
                                    Box(
                                        modifier = Modifier
                                            .fillMaxHeight()
                                            .fillMaxWidth(fraction = progress)
                                            .background(
                                                Brush.horizontalGradient(
                                                    colors = listOf(
                                                        AccentCyan.copy(alpha = 0.06f),
                                                        AccentOrange.copy(alpha = 0.16f)
                                                    )
                                                )
                                            )
                                    )
                                }

                                Row(
                                    modifier = Modifier
                                        .fillMaxSize()
                                        .padding(horizontal = 10.dp),
                                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                                ) {
                                    Row(
                                        modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
                                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                                    ) {
                                        Box(
                                            modifier = Modifier
                                                .size(30.dp)
                                                .clip(RoundedCornerShape(6.dp))
                                                .background(DarkSurface.copy(alpha = 0.85f)),
                                            contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                                        ) {
                                            Icon(
                                                imageVector = Icons.Outlined.LiveTv,
                                                contentDescription = null,
                                                tint = AccentCyan.copy(alpha = 0.65f),
                                                modifier = Modifier.size(16.dp)
                                            )
                                            if (!channel.logoUrl.isNullOrBlank()) {
                                                AsyncImage(
                                                    model = channel.logoUrl,
                                                    contentDescription = channel.name,
                                                    modifier = Modifier
                                                        .fillMaxSize()
                                                        .padding(2.dp),
                                                    contentScale = ContentScale.Fit
                                                )
                                            }
                                        }

                                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(10.dp))

                                        Column(
                                            modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
                                            verticalArrangement = Arrangement.Center
                                        ) {
                                            Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                                                Text(
                                                    text = channel.name,
                                                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium.copy(
                                                        fontSize = 13.5.sp,
                                                        fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold,
                                                        lineHeight = 15.sp
                                                    ),
                                                    color = TextPrimary,
                                                    maxLines = 1,
                                                    overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis,
                                                    modifier = Modifier.weight(1f, fill = false)
                                                )
                                                if (channel.hasArchive) {
                                                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(6.dp))
                                                    Box(
                                                        modifier = Modifier
                                                            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(4.dp))
                                                            .background(AccentOrange.copy(alpha = 0.08f))
                                                            .border(0.75.dp, AccentOrange.copy(alpha = 0.50f), RoundedCornerShape(4.dp))
                                                            .padding(horizontal = 5.dp, vertical = 1.dp)
                                                    ) {
                                                        Text(
                                                            text = if (channel.catchupDays > 0) "↺ ${channel.catchupDays}д" else "↺",
                                                            fontSize = 9.sp,
                                                            fontWeight = FontWeight.Medium,
                                                            color = AccentOrange
                                                        )
                                                    }
                                                }
                                            }
                                            Text(
                                                text = currentProg?.title ?: "Прямой эфир",
                                                style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall.copy(
                                                    fontSize = 11.sp,
                                                    lineHeight = 13.sp
                                                ),
                                                color = if (currentProg != null) TextSecondary else TextSecondary.copy(alpha = 0.65f),
                                                maxLines = 1,
                                                overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis
                                            )
                                        }
                                    }

                                    // Compact TV-Focusable Favorite Toggle Button with explicit FocusBorder
                                    val starScale by animateFloatAsState(if (isStarFocused) 1.12f else 1.0f, label = "star_scale")
                                    val starBg by animateColorAsState(
                                        targetValue = when {
                                            isStarFocused && isFav -> AccentGold.copy(alpha = 0.24f)
                                            isStarFocused -> AccentCyan.copy(alpha = 0.22f)
                                            isFav -> AccentGold.copy(alpha = 0.12f)
                                            else -> Color.Transparent
                                        },
                                        label = "star_bg"
                                    )
                                    val starBorder = when {
                                        isStarFocused && isFav -> AccentGold
                                        isStarFocused -> FocusBorder
                                        isFav -> AccentGold.copy(alpha = 0.45f)
                                        else -> Color.Transparent
                                    }
                                    Box(
                                        modifier = Modifier
                                            .padding(start = 4.dp)
                                            .size(30.dp)
                                            .scale(starScale)
                                            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(8.dp))
                                            .background(starBg)
                                            .then(
                                                if (starBorder != Color.Transparent) {
                                                    Modifier.border(if (isStarFocused) 2.dp else 1.dp, starBorder, RoundedCornerShape(8.dp))
                                                } else Modifier
                                            )
                                            .clickable(
                                                interactionSource = starInteraction,
                                                indication = null
                                            ) {
                                                iptvManager.toggleFavorite(channel.id)
                                            },
                                        contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                                    ) {
                                        Icon(
                                            imageVector = if (isFav) Icons.Rounded.Star else Icons.Outlined.StarOutline,
                                            contentDescription = if (isFav) "В избранном" else "Добавить в избранное",
                                            tint = when {
                                                isFav -> AccentGold
                                                isStarFocused -> AccentCyanBright
                                                else -> TextSecondary
                                            },
                                            modifier = Modifier.size(16.dp)
                                        )
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    if (showLocalFileDialog) {
        Dialog(onDismissRequest = { showLocalFileDialog = false }) {
            Box(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .width(520.dp)
                    .height(440.dp)
                    .clip(RoundedCornerShape(20.dp))
                    .background(DarkCard)
                    .border(1.dp, AccentCyan.copy(alpha = 0.5f), RoundedCornerShape(20.dp))
                    .padding(24.dp)
            ) {
                Column {
                    Row(
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                    ) {
                        Column {
                            Text(
                                text = "Файлы плейлистов (.m3u / .m3u8)",
                                style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleLarge.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold),
                                color = TextPrimary
                            )
                            Text(
                                text = "Найдено на флешке и в памяти: ${localFiles.size}",
                                style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                                color = TextSecondary
                            )
                        }
                        TvFocusCard(
                            onClick = { showLocalFileDialog = false },
                            modifier = Modifier.width(110.dp).height(40.dp)
                        ) {
                            Box(Modifier.fillMaxSize(), contentAlignment = Alignment.Center) {
                                Text("Закрыть", style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium, color = TextSecondary)
                            }
                        }
                    }

                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(16.dp))

                    if (localFiles.isEmpty()) {
                        Box(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(), contentAlignment = Alignment.Center) {
                            Text(
                                text = "Файлы .m3u / .m3u8 не найдены на подключенных накопителях.",
                                style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium,
                                color = TextSecondary
                            )
                        }
                    } else {
                        LazyColumn(
                            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
                            verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp)
                        ) {
                            items(localFiles, key = { it.absolutePath }) { file ->
                                TvFocusCard(
                                    onClick = {
                                        showLocalFileDialog = false
                                        scope.launch { iptvManager.loadFromLocalFile(file) }
                                    },
                                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().height(64.dp)
                                ) {
                                    Row(
                                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize().padding(horizontal = 16.dp),
                                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                                    ) {
                                        Row(
                                            modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
                                            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                                        ) {
                                            Icon(Icons.Outlined.FolderOpen, contentDescription = null, tint = AccentCyan, modifier = Modifier.size(24.dp))
                                            Spacer(Modifier.width(12.dp))
                                            Column {
                                                Text(
                                                    text = file.name,
                                                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium,
                                                    color = TextPrimary,
                                                    maxLines = 1,
                                                    overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis
                                                )
                                                Text(
                                                    text = file.parent ?: "",
                                                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                                                    color = TextSecondary,
                                                    maxLines = 1,
                                                    overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis
                                                )
                                            }
                                        }
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

[ФАЙЛ 45: app/src/main/java/com/replayer/ui/screens/PlayerScreen.kt] (1864 lines)
