@Composable
private fun TvLiveStatusButton(
    isLiveActive: Boolean,
    onClick: () -> Unit,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
) {
    TvControlCapsule(
        onClick = onClick,
        isActive = isLiveActive,
        modifier = modifier
            .height(42.dp)
    ) { isFocused ->
        Row(
            modifier = Modifier.padding(horizontal = 16.dp),
            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(7.dp)
        ) {
            Box(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .size(6.dp)
                    .clip(CircleShape)
                    .background(
                        when {
                            isLiveActive -> AccentCyanBright
                            isFocused -> AccentCyanBright.copy(alpha = 0.75f)
                            else -> TextSecondary.copy(alpha = 0.45f)
                        }
                    )
            )
            Text(
                text = "Эфир",
                style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium.copy(
                    fontSize = 13.sp,
                    fontWeight = if (isLiveActive) FontWeight.Bold else FontWeight.Medium
                ),
                color = when {
                    isLiveActive || isFocused -> AccentCyanBright
                    else -> TextSecondary
                }
            )
        }
    }
}

@Composable
fun TrackSelectionDialog(
    audioTracks: List<AudioTrackInfo>,
    selectedAudioId: String?,
    subtitleTracks: List<SubtitleTrackInfo>,
    selectedSubtitleId: String?,
    onSelectAudio: (AudioTrackInfo) -> Unit,
    onSelectSubtitle: (SubtitleTrackInfo?) -> Unit,
    onDismiss: () -> Unit
) {
    val firstAudioFocusRequester = remember { FocusRequester() }
    val firstSubFocusRequester = remember { FocusRequester() }

    LaunchedEffect(Unit) {
        delay(120)
        try {
            if (audioTracks.isNotEmpty()) {
                firstAudioFocusRequester.requestFocus()
            } else {
                firstSubFocusRequester.requestFocus()
            }
        } catch (_: Exception) {}
    }

    Dialog(
        onDismissRequest = onDismiss,
        properties = DialogProperties(usePlatformDefaultWidth = false)
    ) {
        Box(
            modifier = Modifier
                .width(680.dp)
                .clip(RoundedCornerShape(20.dp))
                .background(DarkCard)
                .border(
                    width = 1.dp,
                    brush = Brush.horizontalGradient(
                        listOf(AccentCyan.copy(alpha = 0.55f), AccentOrange.copy(alpha = 0.45f))
                    ),
                    shape = RoundedCornerShape(20.dp)
                )
                .padding(24.dp)
        ) {
            Column {
                Row(
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                ) {
                    Text(
                        text = "Аудиодорожки и субтитры",
                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleLarge.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold),
                        color = TextPrimary
                    )
                    TvFocusCard(
                        onClick = onDismiss,
                        cornerRadius = 18.dp,
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .width(104.dp)
                            .height(36.dp)
                    ) {
                        Row(
                            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
                            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.Center,
                            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                        ) {
                            Icon(Icons.Outlined.Close, contentDescription = null, tint = TextSecondary, modifier = Modifier.size(15.dp))
                            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(6.dp))
                            Text("Закрыть", style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall, color = TextSecondary)
                        }
                    }
                }

                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(18.dp))

                Row(
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(18.dp)
                ) {
                    // Left Column: Audio Tracks
                    Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                        Row(
                            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp),
                            modifier = Modifier.padding(bottom = 10.dp)
                        ) {
                            Icon(Icons.Outlined.Audiotrack, contentDescription = null, tint = AccentCyanBright, modifier = Modifier.size(17.dp))
                            Text(
                                text = "Звук",
                                style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold),
                                color = AccentCyanBright
                            )
                        }

                        if (audioTracks.isEmpty()) {
                            Text(
                                text = "Дорожки не найдены",
                                style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                                color = TextSecondary,
                                modifier = Modifier.padding(vertical = 12.dp)
                            )
                        } else {
                            LazyColumn(
                                modifier = Modifier
                                    .fillMaxWidth()
                                    .heightIn(max = 280.dp),
                                verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)
                            ) {
                                itemsIndexed(audioTracks) { idx, track ->
                                    val isSelected = track.id == selectedAudioId
                                    TvFocusCard(
                                        onClick = { onSelectAudio(track) },
                                        isSelected = isSelected,
                                        cornerRadius = 12.dp,
                                        modifier = Modifier
                                            .fillMaxWidth()
                                            .height(46.dp)
                                            .then(if (idx == 0) Modifier.focusRequester(firstAudioFocusRequester) else Modifier)
                                    ) {
                                        Row(
                                            modifier = Modifier
                                                .fillMaxSize()
                                                .padding(horizontal = 14.dp),
                                            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                                        ) {
                                            Text(
                                                text = track.label,
                                                style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium,
                                                color = if (isSelected) AccentCyanBright else TextPrimary,
                                                maxLines = 1,
                                                overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis,
                                                modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)
                                            )
                                            if (isSelected) {
                                                Icon(
                                                    imageVector = Icons.Outlined.Check,
                                                    contentDescription = null,
                                                    tint = AccentCyanBright,
                                                    modifier = Modifier.size(17.dp)
                                                )
                                            }
                                        }
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }

                    // Hairline vertical divider
                    Box(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .width(1.dp)
                            .height(260.dp)
                            .background(BorderSubtle)
                    )

                    // Right Column: Subtitles
                    Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                        Row(
                            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp),
                            modifier = Modifier.padding(bottom = 10.dp)
                        ) {
                            Icon(Icons.Outlined.ClosedCaption, contentDescription = null, tint = AccentOrange, modifier = Modifier.size(17.dp))
                            Text(
                                text = "Субтитры",
                                style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold),
                                color = AccentOrange
                            )
                        }

                        LazyColumn(
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .fillMaxWidth()
                                .heightIn(max = 280.dp),
                            verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)
                        ) {
                            item {
                                val isDisabledSelected = selectedSubtitleId == null
                                TvFocusCard(
                                    onClick = { onSelectSubtitle(null) },
                                    isSelected = isDisabledSelected,
                                    cornerRadius = 12.dp,
                                    modifier = Modifier
                                        .fillMaxWidth()
                                        .height(46.dp)
                                        .focusRequester(firstSubFocusRequester)
                                ) {
                                    Row(
                                        modifier = Modifier
                                            .fillMaxSize()
                                            .padding(horizontal = 14.dp),
                                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                                    ) {
                                        Text(
                                            text = "Отключены",
                                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium,
                                            color = if (isDisabledSelected) AccentOrange else TextPrimary,
                                            modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)
                                        )
                                        if (isDisabledSelected) {
                                            Icon(
                                                imageVector = Icons.Outlined.Check,
                                                contentDescription = null,
                                                tint = AccentOrange,
                                                modifier = Modifier.size(17.dp)
                                            )
                                        }
                                    }
                                }
                            }

                            items(subtitleTracks) { track ->
                                val isSelected = track.id == selectedSubtitleId
                                TvFocusCard(
                                    onClick = { onSelectSubtitle(track) },
                                    isSelected = isSelected,
                                    cornerRadius = 12.dp,
                                    modifier = Modifier
                                        .fillMaxWidth()
                                        .height(46.dp)
                                ) {
                                    Row(
                                        modifier = Modifier
                                            .fillMaxSize()
                                            .padding(horizontal = 14.dp),
                                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                                    ) {
                                        Text(
                                            text = track.label,
                                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium,
                                            color = if (isSelected) AccentOrange else TextPrimary,
                                            maxLines = 1,
                                            overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis,
                                            modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)
                                        )
                                        if (isSelected) {
                                            Icon(
                                                imageVector = Icons.Outlined.Check,
                                                contentDescription = null,
                                                tint = AccentOrange,
                                                modifier = Modifier.size(17.dp)
                                            )
                                        }
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
fun EpgAccordionDialog(
    channelTitle: String,
    hasArchive: Boolean,
    playingArchiveProgram: EpgProgram?,
    programs: List<EpgProgram>,
    onSelectProgram: (EpgProgram) -> Unit,
    onDismiss: () -> Unit
) {
    val now = remember { System.currentTimeMillis() }
    val currentProgIndex = remember(programs, playingArchiveProgram) {
        if (playingArchiveProgram != null) {
            val archIdx = programs.indexOfFirst {
                it.startTime == playingArchiveProgram.startTime && it.title == playingArchiveProgram.title
            }
            if (archIdx >= 0) return@remember archIdx
        }
        val idx = programs.indexOfFirst { it.isCurrent(now) }
        if (idx >= 0) idx else programs.indexOfFirst { it.startTime > now }.let { if (it > 0) it - 1 else 0 }
    }
    var focusedIndex by remember { mutableIntStateOf(currentProgIndex.coerceAtLeast(0)) }
    val initialFocusRequester = remember { FocusRequester() }
    val closeFocusRequester = remember { FocusRequester() }
    val listState = rememberLazyListState()

    LaunchedEffect(Unit) {
        delay(120)
        try {
            if (programs.isNotEmpty()) {
                val scrollTarget = (currentProgIndex - 1).coerceAtLeast(0)
                listState.scrollToItem(scrollTarget)
                initialFocusRequester.requestFocus()
            } else {
                closeFocusRequester.requestFocus()
            }
        } catch (_: Exception) {}
    }

    // Ensure newly expanded accordion item's description is never clipped below the viewport
    LaunchedEffect(focusedIndex) {
        delay(235)
        try {
            val visibleInfo = listState.layoutInfo.visibleItemsInfo.find { it.index == focusedIndex }
            val viewportEnd = listState.layoutInfo.viewportEndOffset
            val viewportStart = listState.layoutInfo.viewportStartOffset
            val viewportHeight = viewportEnd - viewportStart
            if (visibleInfo != null && (visibleInfo.offset + visibleInfo.size) > viewportEnd) {
                val targetTopOffset = -(viewportHeight - visibleInfo.size).coerceAtLeast(0)
                listState.animateScrollToItem(
                    index = focusedIndex,
                    scrollOffset = targetTopOffset
                )
            }
        } catch (_: Exception) {}
    }

    Dialog(
        onDismissRequest = onDismiss,
        properties = DialogProperties(usePlatformDefaultWidth = false)
    ) {
        Box(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxWidth(0.72f)
                .widthIn(min = 520.dp, max = 680.dp)
                .fillMaxHeight(0.88f)
                .heightIn(max = 476.dp)
                .clip(RoundedCornerShape(20.dp))
                .background(DarkCard)
                .border(1.dp, AccentCyan.copy(alpha = 0.45f), RoundedCornerShape(20.dp))
                .padding(20.dp)
        ) {
            Column(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
                Row(
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                ) {
                    Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                        Text(
                            text = channelTitle,
                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleLarge.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold),
                            color = TextPrimary,
                            maxLines = 1,
                            overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis
                        )
                        Text(
                            text = if (hasArchive) "Телепрограмма и архив (↑ назад / ↓ вперед)" else "Телепрограмма (без архива)",
                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                            color = TextSecondary
                        )
                    }
                    TvFocusCard(
                        onClick = onDismiss,
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .width(100.dp)
                            .height(36.dp)
                            .then(if (programs.isEmpty()) Modifier.focusRequester(closeFocusRequester) else Modifier)
                    ) {
                        Box(Modifier.fillMaxSize(), contentAlignment = Alignment.Center) {
                            Text("Закрыть", style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall, color = TextSecondary)
                        }
                    }
                }

                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(14.dp))

                if (programs.isEmpty()) {
                    Box(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(), contentAlignment = Alignment.Center) {
                        Text(
                            text = "Для этого канала нет данных программы передач",
                            color = TextSecondary,
                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium
                        )
                    }
                } else {
                    LazyColumn(
                        state = listState,
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
                        contentPadding = PaddingValues(horizontal = 4.dp, vertical = 6.dp),
                        verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)
                    ) {
                        itemsIndexed(programs) { index, prog ->
                            val isItemCurrent = prog.isCurrent(now)
                            val isItemPast = prog.isPast(now)
                            val isExpanded = (index == focusedIndex)
                            val isInitialTarget = (index == currentProgIndex)
                            val isPlayingArchiveItem = playingArchiveProgram != null &&
                                playingArchiveProgram.startTime == prog.startTime &&
                                playingArchiveProgram.title == prog.title

                            val interactionSource = remember { MutableInteractionSource() }
                            val isFocused by interactionSource.collectIsFocusedAsState()

                            val cardBg by animateColorAsState(
                                targetValue = when {
                                    isFocused -> Color(0xFF1F2B38)
                                    isItemCurrent -> Color(0xFF19242E)
                                    else -> DarkSurface
                                },
                                animationSpec = tween(durationMillis = 180),
                                label = "epg_bg"
                            )
                            val cardBorder by animateColorAsState(
                                targetValue = when {
                                    isFocused -> FocusBorder
                                    isItemCurrent -> AccentCyan.copy(alpha = 0.4f)
                                    else -> Color(0x18FFFFFF)
                                },
                                animationSpec = tween(durationMillis = 180),
                                label = "epg_border"
                            )

                            val badgeText: String? = when {
                                isPlayingArchiveItem -> "ИДЁТ"
                                isItemCurrent -> "В ЭФИРЕ"
                                isItemPast && hasArchive -> "АРХИВ"
                                isItemPast -> "ПРОШЛА"
                                else -> null
                            }
                            val badgeColor = when {
                                isPlayingArchiveItem || isItemCurrent -> AccentCyanBright
                                isItemPast && hasArchive -> AccentOrange
                                else -> TextSecondary
                            }

                            Box(
                                modifier = Modifier
                                    .fillMaxWidth()
                                    .padding(horizontal = 2.dp)
                                    .clip(RoundedCornerShape(12.dp))
                                    .background(cardBg)
                                    .border(
                                        width = if (isFocused) 2.dp else 1.dp,
                                        color = cardBorder,
                                        shape = RoundedCornerShape(12.dp)
                                    )
                                    .then(if (isInitialTarget) Modifier.focusRequester(initialFocusRequester) else Modifier)
                                    .onFocusChanged { focusState ->
                                        if (focusState.isFocused) {
                                            focusedIndex = index
                                        }
                                    }
                                    .clickable(
                                        interactionSource = interactionSource,
                                        indication = null,
                                        onClick = { onSelectProgram(prog) }
                                    )
                                    .focusable(interactionSource = interactionSource)
                                    .padding(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = 12.dp)
                            ) {
                                Column(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth()) {
                                    // Consistent single-line header row (never jumps on focus)
                                    Row(
                                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                                    ) {
                                        Text(
                                            text = prog.formattedStartTime(),
                                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold),
                                            color = if (isItemCurrent || isFocused) AccentCyan else TextSecondary,
                                            modifier = Modifier.width(52.dp)
                                        )
                                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(8.dp))
                                        Text(
                                            text = prog.title,
                                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium.copy(
                                                fontWeight = if (isExpanded || isItemCurrent) FontWeight.Bold else FontWeight.Medium
                                            ),
                                            color = TextPrimary,
                                            maxLines = if (isExpanded) 4 else 1,
                                            overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis,
                                            modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)
                                        )
                                        if (badgeText != null) {
                                            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(8.dp))
                                            Box(
                                                modifier = Modifier
                                                    .clip(RoundedCornerShape(6.dp))
                                                    .background(badgeColor.copy(alpha = 0.16f))
                                                    .padding(horizontal = 7.dp, vertical = 2.dp)
                                            ) {
                                                Text(
                                                    text = badgeText,
                                                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold),
                                                    color = badgeColor
                                                )
                                            }
                                        }
                                    }

                                    // Smooth Accordion Expandable Drawer
                                    AnimatedVisibility(
                                        visible = isExpanded,
                                        enter = expandVertically(
                                            animationSpec = tween(durationMillis = 220, easing = FastOutSlowInEasing)
                                        ) + fadeIn(animationSpec = tween(durationMillis = 180)),
                                        exit = shrinkVertically(
                                            animationSpec = tween(durationMillis = 180, easing = FastOutSlowInEasing)
                                        ) + fadeOut(animationSpec = tween(durationMillis = 120))
                                    ) {
                                        Column(
                                            modifier = Modifier
                                                .fillMaxWidth()
                                                .padding(top = 10.dp)
                                        ) {
                                            Text(
                                                text = "${prog.formattedTimeRange()} • ${prog.durationMinutes()} мин.",
                                                style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelMedium,
                                                color = AccentCyan
                                            )

                                            if (isItemCurrent) {
                                                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(6.dp))
                                                Box(
                                                    modifier = Modifier
                                                        .fillMaxWidth()
                                                        .height(5.dp)
                                                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(3.dp))
                                                        .background(Color(0x33FFFFFF))
                                                ) {
                                                    Box(
                                                        modifier = Modifier
                                                            .fillMaxWidth(fraction = prog.progress(now).coerceIn(0f, 1f))
                                                            .fillMaxHeight()
                                                            .background(
                                                                Brush.horizontalGradient(
                                                                    colors = listOf(AccentCyan, AccentGold, AccentOrange)
                                                                )
                                                            )
                                                    )
                                                }
                                            }

                                            if (!prog.description.isNullOrBlank()) {
                                                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(8.dp))
                                                Text(
                                                    text = prog.description,
                                                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall.copy(
                                                        fontSize = 13.sp,
                                                        lineHeight = 18.sp
                                                    ),
                                                    color = TextSecondary
                                                )
                                            }

                                            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(8.dp))
                                            val actionHint = when {
                                                isItemPast && hasArchive -> "▶ Нажмите OK для запуска из архива"
                                                isItemPast -> "Архив отсутствует для этого канала"
                                                isItemCurrent && playingArchiveProgram != null -> "▶ Нажмите OK для возврата в прямой эфир"
                                                isItemCurrent && hasArchive -> "▶ В эфире (OK — смотреть с начала)"
                                                isItemCurrent -> "Сейчас в эфире"
                                                else -> "⏰ Запланировано в эфире"
                                            }
                                            Text(
                                                text = actionHint,
                                                style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall,
                                                color = if ((isItemPast && hasArchive) || isItemCurrent) AccentCyan else TextSecondary
                                            )
                                        }
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

private fun formatTime(ms: Long): String {
    val totalSeconds = (ms / 1000).coerceAtLeast(0)
    val seconds = totalSeconds % 60
    val minutes = (totalSeconds / 60) % 60
    val hours = totalSeconds / 3600
    return if (hours > 0) {
        String.format("%02d:%02d:%02d", hours, minutes, seconds)
    } else {
        String.format("%02d:%02d", minutes, seconds)
    }
}

private fun formatSignedSeekDelta(deltaMs: Long): String {
    val sign = if (deltaMs >= 0L) "+" else "-"
    val totalSec = abs(deltaMs) / 1000L
    val sec = totalSec % 60L
    val min = (totalSec / 60L) % 60L
    val hrs = totalSec / 3600L
    return if (hrs > 0L) {
        String.format("%s%d:%02d:%02d", sign, hrs, min, sec)
    } else {
        String.format("%s%d:%02d", sign, min, sec)
    }
}
